    8:     static std::string disassembleRabbitizerInstruction(RabbitizerInstruction &rabbitizerInst)
    9:     {
   10:         std::string disassembly;
   11:         const size_t bufferSize = RabbitizerInstruction_getSizeForBuffer(&rabbitizerInst, 0, 0);
   12:         if (bufferSize > 0)
   13:         {
   14:             std::vector<char> buffer(bufferSize + 1, '\0');
   15:             RabbitizerInstruction_disassemble(&rabbitizerInst, buffer.data(), nullptr, 0, 0);
   16:             disassembly = buffer.data();
   17:         }
   18: 
   19:         return disassembly;
   20:     }

   94:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesFPR = false;
   95:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVFR = false;
   96:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVIR = false;
   97:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVIC = false;
   98:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesMemory = false;
   99:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = false;
  100: 
  101:         RabbitizerInstruction rabbitizerInst;
  102:         RabbitizerInstructionR5900_init(&rabbitizerInst, rawInstruction, address);
  103:         RabbitizerInstructionR5900_processUniqueId(&rabbitizerInst);
  104: 
  105:         const RabbitizerInstrDescriptor *desc = rabbitizerInst.descriptor;
  106: 
  107:         inst.isBranch = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_isBranch(desc);
  108:         inst.isJump = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_isJump(desc);
  109:         inst.isCall = RabbitizerInstruction_isFunctionCall(&rabbitizerInst);
  110:         inst.isReturn = RabbitizerInstruction_isReturn(&rabbitizerInst) ||
  111:                         rabbitizerInst.uniqueId == RABBITIZER_INSTR_ID_cpu_eret;
  112:         inst.hasDelaySlot = RabbitizerInstruction_hasDelaySlot(&rabbitizerInst);
  113: 
  114:         inst.isLoad = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_doesLoad(desc);
  115:         inst.isStore = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_doesStore(desc);
  116: 
  117:         inst.isMMI = inst.opcode == OPCODE_MMI;
  118:         inst.isVU = inst.opcode == OPCODE_COP2 ||
  119:                     inst.opcode == OPCODE_LWC2 ||
  120:                     inst.opcode == OPCODE_LDC2 ||
  121:                     inst.opcode == OPCODE_SWC2 ||
  122:                     inst.opcode == OPCODE_SDC2;
  123: 
  124:         bool modifiesGpr = false;
  125:         if (RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesRs(desc) && inst.rs != 0)
  126:         {
  127:             modifiesGpr = true;
  128:         }
  129:         if (RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesRt(desc) && inst.rt != 0)
  130:         {
  131:             modifiesGpr = true;
  132:         }
  133:         if (RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesRd(desc) && inst.rd != 0)
  134:         {
  135:             modifiesGpr = true;
  136:         }
  137:         if (RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_doesLink(desc))
  138:         {
  139:             modifiesGpr = true;
  140:         }
  141:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = modifiesGpr;
  142: 
  143:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesFPR = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesFs(desc) ||
  144:                                             RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesFt(desc) ||
  145:                                             RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesFd(desc);
  146: 
  147:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesMemory = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_doesStore(desc);
  148:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_isBranch(desc) ||
  149:                                                 RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_isJump(desc) ||
  150:                                                 RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_isTrap(desc) ||
  151:                                                 RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesHI(desc) ||
  152:                                                 RabbitizerInstrDescriptor_modifiesLO(desc);
  153: 
  154:         if (rabbitizerInst.uniqueId == RABBITIZER_INSTR_ID_cpu_eret)
  155:         {
  156:             inst.isReturn = true;
  157:             inst.hasDelaySlot = false;
  158:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  159:         }
  160: 
  161:         if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_LL || inst.opcode == OPCODE_LLD)
  162:         {
  163:             inst.isLoad = true;
  164:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  165:             if (inst.rt != 0)
  166:             {
  167:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  168:             }
  169:         }
  170: 
  171:         if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SC || inst.opcode == OPCODE_SCD)
  172:         {
  173:             inst.isStore = true;
  174:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  175:             if (inst.rt != 0)
  176:             {
  177:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true; // success flag to rt
  178:             }
  179:         }
  180: 
  181:         if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_COP2)
  182:         {
  183:             decodeCOP2(inst);
  184:         }
  185:         else if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_LWC2 || inst.opcode == OPCODE_LDC2)
  186:         {
  187:             inst.isVU = true;
  188:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVFR = true;
  189:         }
  190:         else if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SWC2 || inst.opcode == OPCODE_SDC2)
  191:         {
  192:             inst.isVU = true;
  193:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesMemory = true;
  194:         }
  195: 
  196:         if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_LQ || inst.opcode == OPCODE_SQ)
  197:         {
  198:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  199:         }
  200: 
  201:         if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SPECIAL)
  202:         {
  203:             decodeSpecial(inst);
  204:         }
  205:         else if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_MMI)
  206:         {
  207:             decodeMMI(inst);
  208:         }
  209: 
  210:         if (inst.isMMI || inst.isVU)
  211:         {
  212:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  213:             inst.vectorInfo.isVector = inst.isVU; // Only VU ops are truly vector
  214:         }
  215: 
  216:         if (includeDisassembly)
  217:         {
  218:             inst.disassembly = disassembleRabbitizerInstruction(rabbitizerInst);
  219:         }
  220: 
  221:         RabbitizerInstructionR5900_destroy(&rabbitizerInst);
  222: 
  223:         return inst;
  224:     }
  225: 
  226:     void R5900Decoder::decodeRType(Instruction &inst) const
  227:     {
  228:         // R-type instructions already have all fields set correctly
  229:         if (inst.rd != 0)
  230:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  231:     }
  232: 
  233:     void R5900Decoder::decodeIType(Instruction &inst) const
  234:     {
  235:         // I-type instructions already have all fields set correctly
  236:         if (!inst.isStore && !inst.isBranch && inst.rt != 0)
  237:         {
  238:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  239:         }
  240:     }
  241: 
  242:     void R5900Decoder::decodeJType(Instruction &inst) const
  243:     {
  244:         // J-type instructions already have all fields set correctly
  245:         inst.isJump = true;
  246:         inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  247:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // PC
  248:         if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_JAL)
  249:         {
  250:             inst.isCall = true;
  251:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true; // $ra (r[31])
  252:         }
  253:     }
  254: 
  255:     void R5900Decoder::decodeSpecial(Instruction &inst) const
  256:     {
  257:         if (inst.rd != 0)
  258:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  259: 
  260:         switch (inst.function)
  261:         {
  262:         case SPECIAL_JR:
  263:             inst.isJump = true;
  264:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  265:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;    // Doesn't modify GPR itself
  266:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // PC
  267:             if (inst.rs == 31)
  268:             {
  269:                 // jr $ra is typically a return
  270:                 inst.isReturn = true;
  271:             }
  272:             break;
  273: 
  274:         case SPECIAL_JALR:
  275:             inst.isJump = true;
  276:             inst.isCall = true;
  277:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  278:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  279:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false; // JALR $zero, $rs is like JR $rs
  280:             else
  281:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  282:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // PC
  283:             break;
  284: 
  285:         case SPECIAL_SYSCALL:
  286:         case SPECIAL_BREAK:
  287:             // Special handling for syscall/break
  288:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;    // No GPR change
  289:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // Changes control flow, potentially registers via handler
  290:             break;
  291: 
  292:         case SPECIAL_MFHI:
  293:         case SPECIAL_MFLO:
  294:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  295:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  296:             break;
  297:         case SPECIAL_MTHI:
  298:         case SPECIAL_MTLO:
  299:             // HI/LO register operations
  300:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;    // Doesn't modify rd
  301:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI/LO
  302:             break;
  303:         case SPECIAL_DIV:
  304:         case SPECIAL_DIVU:
  305:             // Multiplication and division operations
  306:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;    // Doesn't modify rd
  307:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI/LO
  308:             break;
  309:         case SPECIAL_MULT:
  310:         case SPECIAL_MULTU:
  311:             // R5900 MULT/MULTU also write rd when rd != 0.
  312:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = (inst.rd != 0);
  313:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI/LO
  314:             break;
  315:         case SPECIAL_ADD:
  316:         case SPECIAL_ADDU:
  317:         case SPECIAL_SUB:
  318:         case SPECIAL_SUBU:
  319:         case SPECIAL_AND:
  320:         case SPECIAL_OR:
  321:         case SPECIAL_XOR:
  322:         case SPECIAL_NOR:
  323:             // ALU operations
  324:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  325:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  326:             break;
  327: 
  328:         case SPECIAL_SLL:
  329:         case SPECIAL_SRL:
  330:         case SPECIAL_SRA:
  331:         case SPECIAL_SLLV:
  332:         case SPECIAL_SRLV:
  333:         case SPECIAL_SRAV:
  334:             // Shift operations
  335:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  336:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  337:             break;
  338: 
  339:         // 64-bit specific operations
  340:         case SPECIAL_DADD:
  341:         case SPECIAL_DADDU:
  342:         case SPECIAL_DSUB:
  343:         case SPECIAL_DSUBU:
  344:         case SPECIAL_DSLL:
  345:         case SPECIAL_DSRL:
  346:         case SPECIAL_DSRA:
  347:         case SPECIAL_DSLL32:
  348:         case SPECIAL_DSRL32:
  349:         case SPECIAL_DSRA32:
  350:         case SPECIAL_DSLLV:
  351:         case SPECIAL_DSRLV:
  352:         case SPECIAL_DSRAV:
  353:             // 64-bit operations
  354:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  355:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  356:             break;
  357: 
  358:         case SPECIAL_MOVZ:
  359:         case SPECIAL_MOVN:
  360:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  361:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  362:             break;
  363: 
  364:         // Set on Less Than
  365:         case SPECIAL_SLT:
  366:         case SPECIAL_SLTU:
  367:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  368:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  369:             break;
  370: 
  371:         case SPECIAL_TGE:
  372:         case SPECIAL_TGEU:
  373:         case SPECIAL_TLT:
  374:         case SPECIAL_TLTU:
  375:         case SPECIAL_TEQ:
  376:         case SPECIAL_TNE:
  377:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  378:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // Control flow change via trap
  379:             break;
  380: 
  381:         // PS2 Specific (MFSA/MTSA)
  382:         case SPECIAL_MFSA: // Modifies rd GPR
  383:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  384:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  385:             break;
  386:         case SPECIAL_MTSA: // Modifies SA control register
  387:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  388:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // SA reg
  389:             break;
  390: 
  391:         case SPECIAL_SYNC:
  392:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  393:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  394:             break;
  395:         }
  396:     }
  397: 
  398:     void R5900Decoder::decodeRegimm(Instruction &inst) const
  399:     {
  400:         uint32_t rt = inst.rt;
  401: 
  402:         switch (rt)
  403:         {
  404:         case REGIMM_BLTZ:
  405:         case REGIMM_BGEZ:
  406:         case REGIMM_BLTZL:
  407:         case REGIMM_BGEZL:
  408:             // Branches
  409:             inst.isBranch = true;
  410:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  411:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // PC potentially
  412:             break;
  413: 
  414:         case REGIMM_BLTZAL:
  415:         case REGIMM_BGEZAL:
  416:         case REGIMM_BLTZALL:
  417:         case REGIMM_BGEZALL:
  418:             // Branch and Link
  419:             inst.isBranch = true;
  420:             inst.isCall = true;
  421:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  422:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;     // $ra (r[31])
  423:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // PC
  424:             break;
  425: 
  426:         case REGIMM_TGEI:
  427:         case REGIMM_TGEIU:
  428:         case REGIMM_TLTI:
  429:         case REGIMM_TLTIU:
  430:         case REGIMM_TEQI:
  431:         case REGIMM_TNEI:
  432:             // Trap Instructions
  433:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  434:             break;
  435: 
  436:         case REGIMM_MTSAB:
  437:         case REGIMM_MTSAH:
  438:             // PS2 specific MTSAB/MTSAH instructions (for QMFC2/QMTC2)
  439:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  440:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // SA register
  441:             break;
  442: 
  443:         default:
  444:             // Unknown REGIMM instructions
  445:             std::cerr << "Unknown REGIMM instruction: " << std::hex << inst.raw << std::endl;
  446:             break;
  447:         }
  448:     }
  449: 
  450:     void R5900Decoder::decodeMMI(Instruction &inst) const
  451:     {
  452:         inst.isMMI = true;
  453:         inst.isMultimedia = true;
  454:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true; // Most MMI ops write to rd GPR
  455:         if (inst.rd == 0)
  456:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false; // Except if rd is $zero
  457: 
  458:         // The function field is actually determined by the lowest 6 bits (as in R-type)
  459:         uint32_t mmiFunction = inst.function;
  460: 
  461:         uint32_t rs = inst.rs;
  462: 
  463:         if (mmiFunction == MMI_MMI0)
  464:         {
  465:             decodeMMI0(inst);
  466:             return;
  467:         }
  468:         if (mmiFunction == MMI_MMI1)
  469:         {
  470:             decodeMMI1(inst);
  471:             return;
  472:         }
  473:         if (mmiFunction == MMI_MMI2)
  474:         {
  475:             decodeMMI2(inst);
  476:             return;
  477:         }
  478:         if (mmiFunction == MMI_MMI3)
  479:         {
  480:             decodeMMI3(inst);
  481:             return;
  482:         }
  483: 
  484:         switch (mmiFunction)
  485:         {
  486:         case MMI_PLZCW:
  487:             // Parallel leading zero/one count word
  488:             break;
  489:         case MMI_MFHI1:
  490:         case MMI_MFLO1:
  491:             // Move from HI1/LO1 -> rd
  492:             if (inst.rd == 0)
  493:             {
  494:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  495:             }
  496:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = false;
  497:             break;
  498:         case MMI_MTHI1:
  499:         case MMI_MTLO1:
  500:             // Move to HI1/LO1
  501:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  502:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  503:             break;
  504:         case MMI_PSLLH:
  505:         case MMI_PSRLH:
  506:         case MMI_PSRAH:
  507:         case MMI_PSLLW:
  508:         case MMI_PSRLW:
  509:         case MMI_PSRAW:
  510:             // Packed shift instructions; defaults already mark GPR modify (unless rd==0)
  511:             break;
  512: 
  513:         // HI/LO or HI1/LO1 producers/consumers
  514:         case MMI_MADD:
  515:         case MMI_MADDU:
  516:         case MMI_MSUB:
  517:         case MMI_MSUBU:
  518:         case MMI_MADD1:
  519:         case MMI_MADDU1:
  520:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = (inst.rd != 0); // Also writes rd on R5900 I checkd on EE manual
  521:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  522:             break;
  523:         case MMI_MULT1:
  524:         case MMI_MULTU1:
  525:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = (inst.rd != 0); // same
  526:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  527:             break;
  528:         case MMI_DIV1:
  529:         case MMI_DIVU1:
  530:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false; // Writes to HI1/LO1
  531:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  532:             break;
  533:         case MMI_PMTHL:
  534:         case MMI3_PMTHI:
  535:         case MMI3_PMTLO:
  536:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false; // Writes to HI/LO or HI1/LO1
  537:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  538:             break;
  539:         case MMI_PMFHL:
  540:             decodePMFHL(inst);
  541:             break;
  542:         default:
  543:             std::cerr << "Unknown MMI function: " << std::hex << mmiFunction << std::endl;
  544:             break;
  545:         }
  546:     }
  547: 
  548:     void R5900Decoder::decodeMMI0(Instruction &inst) const
  549:     {
  550:         inst.mmiType = 0;
  551:         inst.mmiFunction = inst.sa;
  552: 
  553:         uint32_t sa = inst.sa;
  554: 
  555:         switch (sa)
  556:         {
  557:         case MMI0_PADDW:
  558:         case MMI0_PSUBW:
  559:         case MMI0_PCGTW:
  560:         case MMI0_PMAXW:
  561:         case MMI0_PADDH:
  562:         case MMI0_PSUBH:
  563:         case MMI0_PCGTH:
  564:         case MMI0_PMAXH:
  565:         case MMI0_PADDB:
  566:         case MMI0_PSUBB:
  567:         case MMI0_PCGTB:
  568:             // Arithmetic and comparison operations
  569:             break;
  570: 
  571:         case MMI0_PADDSW:
  572:         case MMI0_PSUBSW:
  573:         case MMI0_PADDSH:
  574:         case MMI0_PSUBSH:
  575:         case MMI0_PADDSB:
  576:         case MMI0_PSUBSB:
  577:             // Saturated arithmetic operations
  578:             break;
  579: 
  580:         case MMI0_PEXTLW:
  581:         case MMI0_PPACW:
  582:         case MMI0_PEXTLH:
  583:         case MMI0_PPACH:
  584:         case MMI0_PEXTLB:
  585:         case MMI0_PPACB:
  586:         case MMI0_PEXT5:
  587:         case MMI0_PPAC5:
  588:             // Data packing/unpacking operations
  589:             break;
  590: 
  591:         default:
  592:             // Unknown MMI0 operation
  593:             break;
  594:         }
  595:     }
  596: 
  597:     void R5900Decoder::decodeMMI1(Instruction &inst) const
  598:     {
  599:         inst.mmiType = 1;
  600:         inst.mmiFunction = inst.sa;
  601: 
  602:         uint32_t subFunction = inst.function & 0x3F;
  603: 
  604:         uint32_t sa = inst.sa;
  605: 
  606:         switch (sa)
  607:         {
  608:         case MMI1_PABSW:
  609:         case MMI1_PABSH:
  610:             // Absolute value operations
  611:             break;
  612: 
  613:         case MMI1_PCEQW:
  614:         case MMI1_PCEQH:
  615:         case MMI1_PCEQB:
  616:             // Equality comparison operations
  617:             break;
  618: 
  619:         case MMI1_PMINW:
  620:         case MMI1_PMINH:
  621:             // Minimum value operations
  622:             break;
  623: 
  624:         case MMI1_PADDUW:
  625:         case MMI1_PSUBUW:
  626:         case MMI1_PEXTUW:
  627:         case MMI1_PADDUH:
  628:         case MMI1_PSUBUH:
  629:         case MMI1_PEXTUH:
  630:         case MMI1_PADDUB:
  631:         case MMI1_PSUBUB:
  632:         case MMI1_PEXTUB:
  633:             // Unsigned arithmetic and extension operations
  634:             break;
  635: 
  636:         case MMI1_QFSRV:
  637:             // Quadword funnel shift right variable
  638:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  639:             break;
  640: 
  641:         default:
  642:             // Unknown MMI1 operation
  643:             break;
  644:         }
  645:     }
  646: 
  647:     void R5900Decoder::decodeMMI2(Instruction &inst) const
  648:     {
  649:         inst.mmiType = 2;
  650:         inst.mmiFunction = inst.sa;
  651: 
  652:         if (inst.sa == MMI2_PMADDW || inst.sa == MMI2_PMSUBW ||
  653:             inst.sa == MMI2_PMULTW || inst.sa == MMI2_PDIVW || inst.sa == MMI2_PDIVBW ||
  654:             inst.sa == MMI2_PMADDH || inst.sa == MMI2_PHMADH ||
  655:             inst.sa == MMI2_PMSUBH || inst.sa == MMI2_PHMSBH || inst.sa == MMI2_PMULTH)
  656:         {
  657:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI/LO
  658:         }
  659: 
  660:         uint32_t sa = inst.sa;
  661: 
  662:         switch (sa)
  663:         {
  664:         case MMI2_PMADDW:
  665:         case MMI2_PMSUBW:
  666:         case MMI2_PMADDH:
  667:         case MMI2_PHMADH:
  668:         case MMI2_PMSUBH:
  669:         case MMI2_PHMSBH:
  670:         case MMI2_PMULTH:
  671:             // Multiply/multiply-add operations
  672:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  673:             break;
  674: 
  675:         case MMI2_PSLLVW:
  676:         case MMI2_PSRLVW:
  677:             // Variable shift operations
  678:             break;
  679: 
  680:         case MMI2_PMFHI:
  681:         case MMI2_PMFLO:
  682:             // Move from HI/LO registers
  683:             break;
  684: 
  685:         case MMI2_PINTH:
  686:             // Interleave half words
  687:             break;
  688: 
  689:         case MMI2_PMULTW:
  690:         case MMI2_PDIVW:
  691:         case MMI2_PDIVBW:
  692:             // Multiply/divide operations
  693:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  694:             break;
  695: 
  696:         case MMI2_PCPYLD:
  697:             // Copy lower doubleword
  698:             break;
  699: 
  700:         case MMI2_PAND:
  701:         case MMI2_PXOR:
  702:             // Logical operations
  703:             break;
  704: 
  705:         case MMI2_PEXEH:
  706:         case MMI2_PREVH:
  707:         case MMI2_PEXEW:
  708:         case MMI2_PROT3W:
  709:             // Data permutation operations
  710:             break;
  711: 
  712:         default:
  713:             // Unknown MMI2 operation
  714:             break;
  715:         }
  716:     }
  717: 
  718:     void R5900Decoder::decodeMMI3(Instruction &inst) const
  719:     {
  720:         inst.mmiType = 3;
  721:         inst.mmiFunction = inst.sa;
  722: 
  723:         uint32_t sa = inst.sa;
  724: 
  725:         switch (sa)
  726:         {
  727:         case MMI3_PMADDUW:
  728:             // Unsigned multiply-add
  729:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  730:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI/LO
  731:             break;
  732: 
  733:         case MMI3_PSRAVW:
  734:             // Packed shift right arithmetic variable word
  735:             break;
  736: 
  737:         case MMI3_PMTHI:
  738:             inst.mmiFunction = MMI3_PMTHI;
  739:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  740:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI register is modified
  741:             break;
  742: 
  743:         case MMI3_PMTLO:
  744:             inst.mmiFunction = MMI3_PMTLO;
  745:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = false;
  746:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // LO register is modified
  747:             break;
  748: 
  749:         case MMI3_PINTEH:
  750:             // Interleave even halfwords
  751:             break;
  752: 
  753:         case MMI3_PMULTUW:
  754:         case MMI3_PDIVUW:
  755:             // Unsigned multiply/divide operations
  756:             inst.isMultimedia = true;
  757:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // HI/LO
  758:             break;
  759: 
  760:         case MMI3_PCPYUD:
  761:             // Copy upper doubleword
  762:             break;
  763: 
  764:         case MMI3_POR:
  765:         case MMI3_PNOR:
  766:             // Logical operations
  767:             break;
  768: 
  769:         case MMI3_PEXCH:
  770:         case MMI3_PCPYH:
  771:         case MMI3_PEXCW:
  772:             // Data permutation operations
  773:             break;
  774: 
  775:         default:
  776:             // Unknown MMI3 operation
  777:             break;
  778:         }
  779:     }
  780: 
  781:     void R5900Decoder::decodeCOP0(Instruction &inst) const
  782:     {
  783:         // COP0 (System Control) instructions
  784:         uint8_t format = inst.rs;
  785:         inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // Assume COP0 always modifies some control state
  786:         if (format == COP0_MF && inst.rt != 0)
  787:         {
  788:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  789:         }
  790:         else if (format == COP0_CO && inst.function == COP0_CO_ERET)
  791:         {
  792:             inst.isReturn = true;
  793:             inst.hasDelaySlot = false;
  794:         } // ERET is special
  795:         else if (format == COP0_BC)
  796:         {
  797:             inst.isBranch = true;
  798:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  799:         }
  800:     }
  801: 
  802:     void R5900Decoder::decodeCOP1(Instruction &inst) const
  803:     {
  804:         uint8_t format = inst.rs;
  805:         if (format == COP1_MF || format == COP1_CF)
  806:         {
  807:             if (inst.rt != 0)
  808:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  809:         }
  810:         else if (format == COP1_BC)
  811:         {
  812:             inst.isBranch = true;
  813:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  814:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  815:         }
  816:         else if (format == COP1_S || format == COP1_W || format == COP1_L)
  817:         {
  818:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesFPR = true;
  819:         }
  820:         if (format == COP1_CT || (format == COP1_S && inst.function >= COP1_S_C_F))
  821:         {
  822:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  823:         } // FCR31
  824:     }
  825: 
  826:     void R5900Decoder::decodeCOP2(Instruction &inst) const
  827:     {
  828:         uint8_t format = inst.rs;
  829:         inst.isVU = true;
  830:         inst.isMultimedia = true;
  831: 
  832:         switch (format)
  833:         {
  834:         case COP2_QMFC2:
  835:         case COP2_CFC2:
  836:             if (inst.rt != 0)
  837:                 inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR = true;
  838:             break;
  839:         case COP2_QMTC2:
  840:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVFR = true;
  841:             break; // Modifies VU state
  842:         case COP2_CTC2:
  843:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  844:             break; // Modifies VU control state
  845:         case COP2_BC:
  846:             inst.isBranch = true;
  847:             inst.hasDelaySlot = true;
  848:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true;
  849:             break;
  850:         case COP2_CO: // VU Macro instructions (format >= 0x10)
  851:         case COP2_CO + 1:
  852:         case COP2_CO + 2:
  853:         case COP2_CO + 3:
  854:         case COP2_CO + 4:
  855:         case COP2_CO + 5:
  856:         case COP2_CO + 6:
  857:         case COP2_CO + 7:
  858:         case COP2_CO + 8:
  859:         case COP2_CO + 9:
  860:         case COP2_CO + 10:
  861:         case COP2_CO + 11:
  862:         case COP2_CO + 12:
  863:         case COP2_CO + 13:
  864:         case COP2_CO + 14:
  865:         case COP2_CO + 15:
  866:         { // Refine based on specific VU function
  867:             const uint8_t special1Func = static_cast<uint8_t>(inst.function & 0x3F);
  868:             const bool isSpecial2 = special1Func >= 0x3C;
  869:             const uint8_t special2Func = static_cast<uint8_t>((((inst.raw >> 6) & 0x1F) << 2) | (inst.raw & 0x3));
  870:             const uint8_t vuFunc = isSpecial2 ? special2Func : special1Func;
  871: 
  872:             inst.vectorInfo.vectorField = static_cast<uint8_t>((inst.raw >> 21) & 0xF);
  873: 
  874:             // Component selectors are encoded in bits 21-24 as L/M for these macro ops.
  875:             if (vuFunc == VU0_S2_VDIV || vuFunc == VU0_S2_VRSQRT ||
  876:                 vuFunc == VU0_S2_VMTIR || vuFunc == VU0_S2_VRINIT || vuFunc == VU0_S2_VRXOR)
  877:             {
  878:                 inst.vectorInfo.fsf = static_cast<uint8_t>((inst.raw >> 21) & 0x3); // L selector
  879:             }
  880:             if (vuFunc == VU0_S2_VDIV || vuFunc == VU0_S2_VSQRT || vuFunc == VU0_S2_VRSQRT)
  881:             {
  882:                 inst.vectorInfo.ftf = static_cast<uint8_t>((inst.raw >> 23) & 0x3); // M selector
  883:             }
  884: 
  885:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVFR = true;     // Default: Modifies Vector Float Reg
  886:             inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl = true; // Default: Modifies Flags/Special Regs (Q, P, I, MAC, Clip...)
  887: 
  888:             if (isSpecial2)
  889:             {
  890:                 switch (vuFunc)
  891:                 {
  892:                 case VU0_S2_VDIV:
  893:                 case VU0_S2_VSQRT:
  894:                 case VU0_S2_VRSQRT:
  895:                     inst.vectorInfo.usesQReg = true;
  896:                     break;
  897:                 case VU0_S2_VMTIR:
  898:                     inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVFR = false;
  899:                     inst.modificationInfo.modifiesVIC = true;
  900:                     break;
[SOURCE TRUNCATED AT 900 LINES]

  229:         MMI_MADDU = 0x01, // Multiply Add Unsigned Word (to LO/HI)
  230:         MMI_MSUB = 0x02,  // Multiply Subtract Word (Not listed in provided doc table, but valid MMI)
  231:         MMI_MSUBU = 0x03, // Multiply Subtract Unsigned Word (Not listed in provided doc table, but valid MMI)
  232:         MMI_PLZCW = 0x04, // Parallel Leading Zero/One Count Word
  233: 
  234:         MMI_MMI0 = 0x08, // Group MMI0 instructions (see MMI0Functions)
  235:         MMI_MMI2 = 0x09, // Group MMI2 instructions (see MMI2Functions)
  236: 
  237:         MMI_MFHI1 = 0x10, // Move From HI1 Register
  238:         MMI_MTHI1 = 0x11, // Move To HI1 Register
  239:         MMI_MFLO1 = 0x12, // Move From LO1 Register
  240:         MMI_MTLO1 = 0x13, // Move To LO1 Register
  241: 
  242:         MMI_MULT1 = 0x18,  // Multiply Word to LO1/HI1
  243:         MMI_MULTU1 = 0x19, // Multiply Unsigned Word to LO1/HI1
  244:         MMI_DIV1 = 0x1A,   // Divide Word using LO1/HI1
  245:         MMI_DIVU1 = 0x1B,  // Divide Unsigned Word using LO1/HI1
  246: 
  247:         MMI_MADD1 = 0x20,  // Multiply Add Word to LO1/HI1
  248:         MMI_MADDU1 = 0x21, // Multiply Add Unsigned Word to LO1/HI1
  249: 
  250:         MMI_MMI1 = 0x28, // Group MMI1 instructions (see MMI1Functions)
  251:         MMI_MMI3 = 0x29, // Group MMI3 instructions (see MMI3Functions)
  252: 
  253:         MMI_PMFHL = 0x30, // Parallel Move From HI/LO Register (see PMFHLFunctions for 'sa' field)
  254:         MMI_PMTHL = 0x31, // Parallel Move To HI/LO Register (see PMFHLFunctions for 'sa' field)
  255: 
  256:         MMI_PSLLH = 0x34, // Parallel Shift Left Logical Halfword
  257: 
  258:         MMI_PSRLH = 0x36, // Parallel Shift Right Logical Halfword
  259:         MMI_PSRAH = 0x37, // Parallel Shift Right Arithmetic Halfword
  260: 
  261:         MMI_PSLLW = 0x3C, // Parallel Shift Left Logical Word
  262: 
  263:         MMI_PSRLW = 0x3E, // Parallel Shift Right Logical Word
  264:         MMI_PSRAW = 0x3F  // Parallel Shift Right Arithmetic Word
  265:     };
  266: 
  267:     // PS2-specific MMI0 Sub-Function Field (bits 0-5 within MMI_MMI0 group)
  268:     enum MMI0Functions
  269:     {
  270:         MMI0_PADDW = 0x00,
  271:         MMI0_PSUBW = 0x01,
  272:         MMI0_PCGTW = 0x02,
  273:         MMI0_PMAXW = 0x03,
  274:         MMI0_PADDH = 0x04,
  275:         MMI0_PSUBH = 0x05,

  279:         MMI0_PSUBB = 0x09,
  280:         MMI0_PCGTB = 0x0A,
  281:         MMI0_PADDSW = 0x10,
  282:         MMI0_PSUBSW = 0x11,
  283:         MMI0_PEXTLW = 0x12,
  284:         MMI0_PPACW = 0x13,
  285:         MMI0_PADDSH = 0x14,
  286:         MMI0_PSUBSH = 0x15,
  287:         MMI0_PEXTLH = 0x16,
  288:         MMI0_PPACH = 0x17,
  289:         MMI0_PADDSB = 0x18,
  290:         MMI0_PSUBSB = 0x19,

  292:         MMI0_PPACB = 0x1B,
  293:         MMI0_PEXT5 = 0x1E,
  294:         MMI0_PPAC5 = 0x1F
  295:     };
  296:

  346:

  348:     enum MMI3Functions
  349:     {

  351:         MMI3_PSRAVW = 0x03,

  364: 
  365:     // PMFHL/PMTHL Sub-Function Field ('sa' field, bits 10-6)
  366:     enum PMFHLFunctions
  367:     {
  368:         PMFHL_LW = 0x00,  // Load Word / Lower Word
  369:         PMFHL_UW = 0x01,  // Upper Word
  370:         PMFHL_SLW = 0x02, // Signed Lower Word
  371:         PMFHL_LH = 0x03,  // Load Halfword / Lower Halfword
  372:         PMFHL_SH = 0x04   // Store Halfword / Signed Upper Halfword? (Doc name varies)
  373:     };
  374: 
  375:     // COP0 Format Field ('fmt' or 'rs' field, bits 25-21) for OPCODE_COP0
  376:     enum COP0Format
  377:     {
  378:         COP0_MF = 0x00, // Move From COP0 - MFC0 (fmt=00000)
  379:         COP0_MT = 0x04, // Move To COP0 - MTC0 (fmt=00100)
  380:         COP0_BC = 0x08, // BC0 group (fmt=01000) (see Cop0BranchCondition)
  381:         COP0_CO = 0x10  // COP0 Operation group (fmt=10000) (see Cop0CoFunctions)
  382:     };
  383: 
  384:     // COP0 CO Function Field (bits 5-0) for COP0_CO Format
  385:     enum Cop0CoFunctions
  386:     {
  387:         COP0_CO_TLBR = 0x01,  // TLB Read
  388:         COP0_CO_TLBWI = 0x02, // TLB Write Indexed
  389: 
  390:         COP0_CO_TLBWR = 0x06, // TLB Write Random
  391: 
  392:         COP0_CO_TLBP = 0x08, // TLB Probe
  393: 
  394:         COP0_CO_ERET = 0x18, // Return from Exception
  395: 
  396:         COP0_CO_EI = 0x38, // Enable Interrupts
  397:         COP0_CO_DI = 0x39  // Disable Interrupts
  398:     };
  399: 
  400:     // COP0 BC Condition Field ('fmt' or 'rt' field, bits 20-16) for COP0_BC Format
  401:     enum Cop0BranchCondition
  402:     {
  403:         COP0_BC_BCF = 0x00,  // Branch on Condition False
  404:         COP0_BC_BCT = 0x01,  // Branch on Condition True
  405:         COP0_BC_BCFL = 0x02, // Branch on Condition False Likely
  406:         COP0_BC_BCTL = 0x03, // Branch on Condition True Likely
  407:     };
  408: 
  409:     // COP0 Register Numbers (used with MFC0/MTC0)
  410:     enum COP0Reg
  411:     {
  412:         COP0_REG_INDEX = 0,    // Index into TLB array
  413:         COP0_REG_RANDOM = 1,   // Randomly generated index into TLB array
  414:         COP0_REG_ENTRYLO0 = 2, // Low half of TLB entry for even-numbered virtual pages
  415:         COP0_REG_ENTRYLO1 = 3, // Low half of TLB entry for odd-numbered virtual pages
  416:         COP0_REG_CONTEXT = 4,  // Context
  417:         COP0_REG_PAGEMASK = 5, // TLB page size mask
  418:         COP0_REG_WIRED = 6,    // Controls which TLB entries are affected by random replacement
  419: 
  420:         COP0_REG_BADVADDR = 8, // Virtual address of most recent address-related exception
  421:         COP0_REG_COUNT = 9,    // Timer count
  422:         COP0_REG_ENTRYHI = 10, // High half of TLB entry
  423:         COP0_REG_COMPARE = 11, // Timer compare value
  424:         COP0_REG_STATUS = 12,  // Processor status and control
  425:         COP0_REG_CAUSE = 13,   // Cause of last exception
  426:         COP0_REG_EPC = 14,     // Exception program counter
  427:         COP0_REG_PRID = 15,    // Processor identification and revision
  428:         COP0_REG_CONFIG = 16,  // Configuration register
  429: 
  430:         COP0_REG_BADPADDR = 23, // Bad physical address
  431:         COP0_REG_DEBUG = 24,    // Debug register
  432:         COP0_REG_PERF = 25,     // Performance counter
  433: 
  434:         COP0_REG_TAGLO = 28,   // Cache TagLo (I-Cache & D-Cache)
  435:         COP0_REG_TAGHI = 29,   // High bits of cache tag ( Cache TagHi )
  436:         COP0_REG_ERROREPC = 30 // Error exception program counter
  437:     };
  438: 
  439:     // COP1 (FPU) Format Field ('fmt' or 'rs' field, bits 25-21) for OPCODE_COP1
  440:     enum Cop1Format
  441:     {
  442:         COP1_MF = 0x00, // Move From FPU Register
  443:         COP1_CF = 0x02, // Move From FPU Control Register
  444:         COP1_MT = 0x04, // Move To FPU Register
  445:         COP1_CT = 0x06, // Move To FPU Control Register
  446:         COP1_BC = 0x08, // Branch on FPU Condition (see Cop1BranchCondition)
  447: 
  448:         COP1_D = 0x11, // Standard MIPS Double format; EE FPU is single-precision, (unused on PS2 FPU?)
  449:         COP1_L = 0x15, // Long (64-bit integer) Operation (unused on PS2 FPU?)
  450: 
  451:         COP1_S = 0x10, // Single Precision Operation (see Cop1FunctionsS)
  452:         COP1_W = 0x14  // Word (integer) Operation (see Cop1FunctionsW)
  453:     };
  454: 
  455:     // COP1 Function Field (bits 5-0) for COP1_S Format (Single-Precision)
  456:     enum Cop1FunctionsS
  457:     {
  458:         COP1_S_ADD = 0x00,  // ADD.S
  459:         COP1_S_SUB = 0x01,  // SUB.S
  460:         COP1_S_MUL = 0x02,  // MUL.S
  461:         COP1_S_DIV = 0x03,  // DIV.S
  462:         COP1_S_SQRT = 0x04, // SQRT.S
  463:         COP1_S_ABS = 0x05,  // ABS.S
  464:         COP1_S_MOV = 0x06,  // MOV.S
  465:         COP1_S_NEG = 0x07,  // NEG.S
  466: 
  467:         COP1_S_ROUND_L = 0x08, // ROUND.L.S - Requires 64-bit int dest, likely unused
  468:         COP1_S_TRUNC_L = 0x09, // TRUNC.L.S - Requires 64-bit int dest, likely unused
  469:         COP1_S_CEIL_L = 0x0A,  // CEIL.L.S  - Requires 64-bit int dest, likely unused
  470:         COP1_S_FLOOR_L = 0x0B, // FLOOR.L.S - Requires 64-bit int dest, likely unused
  471:         COP1_S_ROUND_W = 0x0C, // ROUND.W.S
  472:         COP1_S_TRUNC_W = 0x0D, // TRUNC.W.S
  473:         COP1_S_CEIL_W = 0x0E,  // CEIL.W.S
  474:         COP1_S_FLOOR_W = 0x0F, // FLOOR.W.S
  475: 
  476:         // --- Additional Arithmetic/Functions (from FPU.S table) ---
  477:         COP1_S_RSQRT = 0x16, // RSQRT.S (Reciprocal Square Root)
  478:         COP1_S_ADDA = 0x18,  // ADDA.S (Add Accumulator)
  479:         COP1_S_SUBA = 0x19,  // SUBA.S (Subtract Accumulator)
  480:         COP1_S_MULA = 0x1A,  // MULA.S (Multiply Accumulator)
  481:         COP1_S_MADD = 0x1C,  // MADD.S (Multiply Add)
  482:         COP1_S_MSUB = 0x1D,  // MSUB.S (Multiply Subtract)
  483:         COP1_S_MADDA = 0x1E, // MADDA.S (Multiply Add Accumulator)
  484:         COP1_S_MSUBA = 0x1F, // MSUBA.S (Multiply Subtract Accumulator)
  485: 
  486:         // --- Conversions (from FPU.S table) ---
  487:         COP1_S_CVT_D = 0x21, // CVT.D.S - Requires double-precision FPU
  488:         COP1_S_CVT_W = 0x24, // CVT.W.S (Convert to Word)
  489:         COP1_S_CVT_L = 0x25, // CVT.L.S - Requires 64-bit int dest, likely unused
  490: 
  491:         // --- Min/Max (from FPU.S table) ---
  492:         COP1_S_MAX = 0x28, // MAX.S
  493:         COP1_S_MIN = 0x29, // MIN.S
  494: 
  495:         COP1_S_C_F = 0x30,    // C.F.S (False)
  496:         COP1_S_C_UN = 0x31,   // C.UN.S (Unordered)
  497:         COP1_S_C_EQ = 0x32,   // C.EQ.S (Equal)
  498:         COP1_S_C_UEQ = 0x33,  // C.UEQ.S (Unordered or Equal)
  499:         COP1_S_C_OLT = 0x34,  // C.OLT.S (Ordered Less Than)
  500:         COP1_S_C_ULT = 0x35,  // C.ULT.S (Unordered or Less Than)
  501:         COP1_S_C_OLE = 0x36,  // C.OLE.S (Ordered Less Than or Equal)
  502:         COP1_S_C_ULE = 0x37,  // C.ULE.S (Unordered or Less Than or Equal)
  503:         COP1_S_C_SF = 0x38,   // C.SF.S (Signaling False)
  504:         COP1_S_C_NGLE = 0x39, // C.NGLE.S (Not Greater or Less or Equal - Unordered)
  505:         COP1_S_C_SEQ = 0x3A,  // C.SEQ.S (Signaling Equal)
  506:         COP1_S_C_NGL = 0x3B,  // C.NGL.S (Not Greater or Less - Unordered or Equal)
  507:         COP1_S_C_LT = 0x3C,   // C.LT.S (Signaling Less Than)
  508:         COP1_S_C_NGE = 0x3D,  // C.NGE.S (Not Greater or Equal - Unordered or Less Than)
  509:         COP1_S_C_LE = 0x3E,   // C.LE.S (Signaling Less Than or Equal)
  510:         COP1_S_C_NGT = 0x3F,  // C.NGT.S (Not Greater Than - Unordered or Less Than or Equal)
  511:     };
  512: 
  513:     // COP1 Function Field (bits 5-0) for COP1_W Format (Word/Integer source)
  514:     enum Cop1FunctionsW
  515:     {
  516:         COP1_W_CVT_S = 0x20, // CVT.S.W (Convert Word Integer to Single Float)
  517:                              // 0x21 --- (Standard MIPS: CVT.D.W - requires double-precision)
  518:                              // 0x24 --- (Standard MIPS: CVT.W.W - Nop?)
  519:                              // 0x25 --- (Standard MIPS: CVT.L.W - requires 64-bit int dest)
  520:     };
  521: 
  522:     // COP1 BC Condition Field ('fmt' or 'rt' field, bits 20-16) for COP1_BC Format
  523:     enum Cop1BranchCondition
  524:     {
  525:         COP1_BC_BCF = 0x00,  // Branch on FPU Condition False (BC1F)
  526:         COP1_BC_BCT = 0x01,  // Branch on FPU Condition True (BC1T)
  527:         COP1_BC_BCFL = 0x02, // Branch on FPU Condition False Likely (BC1FL)

  529:     };
  530: 
  531:     // COP2 (VU0 Macro) Format Field ('fmt' or 'rs' field, bits 25-21) for OPCODE_COP2
  532:     enum Cop2Format
  533:     {
  534:         // Note: Standard MIPS MFC2/MTC2 use fmt 0x00/0x04, EE uses QMFC2/QMTC2
  535:         COP2_QMFC2 = 0x01, // QMFC2 (fmt=00001) - Load Quadword From Coprocessor 2
  536:         COP2_CFC2 = 0x02,  // CFC2 (fmt=00010) - Load Control Word From Coprocessor 2
  537:         COP2_QMTC2 = 0x05, // QMTC2 (fmt=00101) - Store Quadword To Coprocessor 2
  538:         COP2_CTC2 = 0x06,  // CTC2 (fmt=00110) - Store Control Word To Coprocessor 2
  539:         COP2_BC = 0x08,    // BC2 group (fmt=01000) (see Cop2BranchCondition)
  540:         COP2_CO = 0x10,    // VU0 Macro Operation group (fmt=1xxxx)
  541:     };
  542:

  546:         COP2_BC_BCF = 0x00,  // Branch on VU0 Condition False (BC2F)
  547:         COP2_BC_BCT = 0x01,  // Branch on VU0 Condition True (BC2T)
  548:         COP2_BC_BCFL = 0x02, // Branch on VU0 Condition False Likely (BC2FL)
  549:         COP2_BC_BCTL = 0x03, // Branch on VU0 Condition True Likely (BC2TL)
  550:     };

  626:         VU0_S2_VMADDAw = 0x0B,

  628:         VU0_S2_VMSUBAy = 0x0D,
  629:         VU0_S2_VMSUBAz = 0x0E,

  631:         VU0_S2_VITOF0 = 0x10,
  632:         VU0_S2_VITOF4 = 0x11,
  633:         VU0_S2_VITOF12 = 0x12,
  634:         VU0_S2_VITOF15 = 0x13,
  635:         VU0_S2_VFTOI0 = 0x14,
  636:         VU0_S2_VFTOI4 = 0x15,
  637:         VU0_S2_VFTOI12 = 0x16,
  638:         VU0_S2_VFTOI15 = 0x17,
  639:         VU0_S2_VMULAx = 0x18,
  640:         VU0_S2_VMULAy = 0x19,
  641:         VU0_S2_VMULAz = 0x1A,
  642:         VU0_S2_VMULAw = 0x1B,
  643:         VU0_S2_VMULAq = 0x1C,
  644:         VU0_S2_VABS = 0x1D,
  645:         VU0_S2_VMULAi = 0x1E,
  646:         VU0_S2_VCLIPw = 0x1F,
  647:         VU0_S2_VADDAq = 0x20,

  667:         VU0_S2_VSQD = 0x37,

  682:     // // VU0 macro instruction function codes (subset - there are many more)
  683:     // enum VU0MacroFunctions
  684:     // {
  685:     //     VU0_VADD = 0x00,
  686:     //     VU0_VSUB = 0x01,
  687:     //     VU0_VMUL = 0x02,
  688:     //     VU0_VDIV = 0x03,
  689:     //     VU0_VSQRT = 0x04,
  690:     //     VU0_VRSQRT = 0x05,
  691:     //     VU0_VMULQ = 0x06,
  692:     //     VU0_VIADD = 0x10,
  693:     //     VU0_VISUB = 0x11,
  694:     //     VU0_VIADDI = 0x12,
  695:     //     VU0_VIAND = 0x13,
  696:     //     VU0_VIOR = 0x14,
  697:     //     VU0_VILWR = 0x15,
  698:     //     VU0_VISWR = 0x16,
  699:     //     VU0_VCALLMS = 0x20,
  700:     //     VU0_VCALLMSR = 0x21,
  701:     //     VU0_VRGET = 0x3F,   // Get VU0 R register
  702:     //     VU0_VSUB_XYZ = 0x38 // Subtract with component selection
  703:     // };
  704: 
  705:     // enum VU0SpecialFormats
  706:     // {
  707:     //     VU0_BC2F = 0x11,   // Branch on VU0 condition false
  708:     //     VU0_MTVUCF = 0x13, // Move To VU0 control/flag register
  709:     //     VU0_CTCVU = 0x18,  // Control To VU0 with specific functionality
  710:     //     VU0_VMTIR = 0x1C,  // Move To VU0 I Register
  711:     //     VU0_VCLIP = 0x1E,  // VU0 Clipping operation
  712:     //     VU0_VLDQ = 0x1F    // VU0 Load/Store Quad with Decrement
  713:     // };
  714: 
  715:     // VU0 COP2 control register numbers used by CFC2/CTC2.
  716:     // Registers 0..15 address VI0..VI15 directly.
  717:     enum VU0ControlRegisters
  718:     {
  719:         VU0_CR_STATUS = 16,
  720:         VU0_CR_MAC = 17,
  721:         VU0_CR_CLIP = 18,
  722:         VU0_CR_R = 20,
  723:         VU0_CR_I = 21,
  724:         VU0_CR_Q = 22,
  725:         VU0_CR_TPC = 26,
  726:         VU0_CR_CMSAR0 = 27,
  727:         VU0_CR_FBRST = 28,
  728:         VU0_CR_VPU_STAT = 29,
  729:         VU0_CR_CMSAR1 = 31
  730:     };
  731:     enum VU0OPSFunctions
  732:     {
  733:         VU0OPS_QMFC2_NI = 0x00, // Non-incrementing QMFC2
  734:         VU0OPS_QMFC2_I = 0x01,  // Incrementing QMFC2
  735:         VU0OPS_QMTC2_NI = 0x02, // Non-incrementing QMTC2
  736:         VU0OPS_QMTC2_I = 0x03,  // Incrementing QMTC2
  737:         VU0OPS_VMFIR = 0x04,    // Move From Integer Register
  738:         VU0OPS_VXITOP = 0x08,   // Execute Interrupt on VU0
  739:         VU0OPS_VWAITQ = 0x3B,   // Wait for Q register operations to complete
  740:         VU0OPS_VMFHL = 0x1C,    // Move from High / Low(similar to PMFHL)
  741:         VU0OPS_VMTIR = 0x3C     // Move to VU0 I Register with function code
  742:     };
  743: 
  744:     enum VU0OPSFunctionsExtra
  745:     {
  746:         VU0_VCALLMS_DIRECT = 0x00,
  747:         VU0_VCALLMS_REG = 0x01
  748:     };
  749: 
  750:     enum VU0SpecialMasks
  751:     {
  752:         VU0_FIELD_MASK = 0xF,  // Mask for vector field selection
  753:         VU0_FIELD_SHIFT = 21,  // Shift amount for field selection
  754:         VU0_STORE_BIT = 0x80,  // Bit indicating store operation
  755:         VU0_SUBOP_MASK = 0x1F, // Mask for sub-operation code
  756:         VU0_SUBOP_SHIFT = 6,   // Shift amount for sub-operation
  757:         VU0_SQC0 = 0x8         // SQC0 instruction code
  758:     };
  759: 
  760:     enum VU0FormatsExtra
  761:     {
  762:         VU0_FMT_MACRO_MOVE = 0x1, // Move between VU registers format
  763:         VU0_FMT_VIF_STATUS = 0x5, // VIF status operations
  764:         VU0_FMT_VCALLMS = 0x14,   // VU0 microprogram call format
  765:         VU0_FMT_LQSQ_COP0 = 0x1B  // Load/store quad VU0 format
  766:     };
  767: 
  768: // Instruction decoding helper macros
  769: #define OPCODE(inst) ((inst >> 26) & 0x3F)
  770: #define RS(inst) ((inst >> 21) & 0x1F)
  771: #define RT(inst) ((inst >> 16) & 0x1F)
  772: #define RD(inst) ((inst >> 11) & 0x1F)
  773: #define SA(inst) ((inst >> 6) & 0x1F)
  774: #define FUNCTION(inst) ((inst) & 0x3F)
  775: #define IMMEDIATE(inst) ((inst) & 0xFFFF)
  776: #define SIMMEDIATE(inst) ((int32_t)(int16_t)((inst) & 0xFFFF))
  777: #define TARGET(inst) ((inst) & 0x3FFFFFF)
  778: #define COP_FUNCT(inst) ((uint8_t)((inst) & 0x3F))                    // Function field for COPz CO instructions
  779: #define FPU_FMT(inst) ((uint8_t)(((inst) >> 21) & 0x1F))              // Format field for FPU instructions
  780: #define FT(inst) RT(inst)                                             // Target FPU register
  781: #define FS(inst) RD(inst)                                             // Source FPU register
  782: #define FD(inst) SA(inst)                                             // Destination FPU register
  783: #define VU_I(inst) ((inst >> 25) & 0x1)                               // VU Upper I bit
  784: #define VU_E(inst) ((inst >> 24) & 0x1)                               // VU Upper E bit
  785: #define VU_M(inst) ((inst >> 23) & 0x1)                               // VU Upper M bit (VU0 only)
  786: #define VU_D(inst) ((inst >> 22) & 0x1)                               // VU Upper D bit
  787: #define VU_T(inst) ((inst >> 21) & 0x1)                               // VU Upper T bit
  788: #define VU_DEST(inst) ((uint8_t)(((inst) >> 16) & 0xF))               // VU Destination mask
  789: #define VU_FSF(inst) ((uint8_t)(((inst) >> 10) & 0x3))                // VU Source Field F
  790: #define VU_FTF(inst) ((uint8_t)(((inst) >> 8) & 0x3))                 // VU Target Field F
  791: #define VU_FD(inst) ((uint8_t)(((inst) >> 11) & 0x1F))                // VU Destination Vector Register
  792: #define VU_FS(inst) ((uint8_t)(((inst) >> 6) & 0x1F))                 // VU Source Vector Register
  793: #define VU_FT(inst) ((uint8_t)((inst) & 0x1F))                        // VU Target Vector Register
  794: #define VU_IS(inst) RT(inst)                                          // VU Source Integer Register
  795: #define VU_IT(inst) RD(inst)                                          // VU Target Integer Register
  796: #define VU_ID(inst) SA(inst)                                          // VU Destination Integer Register
  797: #define VU_IMM5(inst) ((uint8_t)((inst >> 6) & 0x1F))                 // VU 5-bit immediate
  798: #define VU_IMM11(inst) ((uint16_t)((inst) & 0x7FF))                   // VU 11-bit immediate
  799: #define VU_SIMM11(inst) ((int16_t)(((inst & 0x7FF) ^ 0x400) - 0x400)) // VU 11-bit signed immediate
  800: #define VU_IMM15(inst) ((uint16_t)((inst) & 0x7FFF))                  // VU 15-bit immediate
  801: 
  802: } // namespace ps2recomp
  803: 
  804: #endif // PS2RECOMP_INSTRUCTIONS_H

   11: namespace ps2recomp
   12: {
   13:     namespace
   14:     {
   15:         std::string addressLiteral(uint32_t address)
   16:         {
   17:             return fmt::format("0x{:X}u", address);
   18:         }
   19: 
   20:         uint32_t memoryAccessSize(int width)
   21:         {
   22:             return static_cast<uint32_t>(width / 8);
   23:         }
   24: 
   25:         std::string memoryValueType(int width)
   26:         {
   27:             switch (width)
   28:             {
   29:             case 8:
   30:                 return "uint8_t";
   31:             case 16:
   32:                 return "uint16_t";
   33:             case 32:
   34:                 return "uint32_t";
   35:             case 64:
   36:                 return "uint64_t";
   37:             default:
   38:                 return "";
   39:             }
   40:         }
   41: 
   42:         std::string genFastWrite(int width, uint32_t address, const std::string &val)
   43:         {
   44:             const std::string addr = addressLiteral(address);
   45:             if (width == 128)
   46:             {
   47:                 return fmt::format(
   48:                     "do {{ __m128i _value = ({}); "
   49:                     "const uint64_t _lo = static_cast<uint64_t>(PS2_EXTRACT_EPI64_0(_value)); "
   50:                     "const uint64_t _hi = static_cast<uint64_t>(PS2_EXTRACT_EPI64_1(_value)); "
   51:                     "ps2TraceGuestWrite(rdram, {}, 16u, _lo, _hi, \"WRITE128\", ctx); "
   52:                     "FAST_WRITE128({}, _value); }} while (0)",
   53:                     val, addr, addr);
   54:             }
   55: 
   56:             const std::string valueType = memoryValueType(width);
   57:             return fmt::format(
   58:                 "do {{ {} _value = static_cast<{}>({}); "
   59:                 "ps2TraceGuestWrite(rdram, {}, {}u, _value, 0u, \"WRITE{}\", ctx); "
   60:                 "FAST_WRITE{}({}, _value); }} while (0)",
   61:                 valueType, valueType, val, addr, memoryAccessSize(width), width, width, addr);
   62:         }
   63:     }

   68:     }

   70:     MemoryAccessHint InstructionTranslator::effectiveMemoryHintFor(const Instruction &inst, const MemoryAccessHint &memoryHint) const
   71:     {
   72:         // TODO disable for now since it causing issues with some games.
   73:         return memoryHint;

   75: 
   76:     std::string InstructionTranslator::translateMemoryRead(const Instruction &inst,
   77:                                                            const MemoryAccessHint &memoryHint,
   78:                                                            int width,
   79:                                                            const std::string &addr) const
   80:     {
   81:         if (memoryHint.hasAddress)
   82:         {
   83:             const uint32_t resolvedAddress = memoryHint.address;
   84:             const std::string resolvedAddressExpr = addressLiteral(resolvedAddress);
   85:             if (inst.isMmio || Ps2IsSpecialAddress(resolvedAddress))
   86:             {
   87:                 return fmt::format("runtime->Load{}(rdram, ctx, {})", width, resolvedAddressExpr);
   88:             }
   89:             return fmt::format("FAST_READ{}({})", width, resolvedAddressExpr);
   90:         }
   91: 
   92:         if (inst.isMmio)
   93:         {
   94:             return fmt::format("runtime->Load{}(rdram, ctx, {})", width, addr);
   95:         }
   96:         return fmt::format("READ{}({})", width, addr);
   97:     }
   98: 
   99:     std::string InstructionTranslator::translateMemoryWrite(const Instruction &inst,
  100:                                                             const MemoryAccessHint &memoryHint,
  101:                                                             int width,
  102:                                                             const std::string &addr,
  103:                                                             const std::string &value) const
  104:     {
  105:         if (memoryHint.hasAddress)
  106:         {
  107:             const uint32_t resolvedAddress = memoryHint.address;
  108:             const std::string resolvedAddressExpr = addressLiteral(resolvedAddress);
  109:             if (inst.isMmio || Ps2IsSpecialAddress(resolvedAddress))
  110:             {
  111:                 return fmt::format("runtime->Store{}(rdram, ctx, {}, {})", width, resolvedAddressExpr, value);
  112:             }
  113:             return genFastWrite(width, resolvedAddress, value);
  114:         }
  115: 
  116:         if (inst.isMmio)
  117:         {
  118:             return fmt::format("runtime->Store{}(rdram, ctx, {}, {})", width, addr, value);
  119:         }
  120:         return fmt::format("WRITE{}({}, {})", width, addr, value);
  121:     }
  122: 
  123:     std::string InstructionTranslator::translate(const Instruction &inst, const MemoryAccessHint &memoryHint)
  124:     {
  125:         if (inst.isMMI)
  126:         {
  127:             return m_codeGenerator.translateMMIInstruction(inst);
  128:         }
  129: 
  130:         const MemoryAccessHint effectiveMemoryHint = effectiveMemoryHintFor(inst, memoryHint);
  131: 
  132:         auto genRead = [&](int width, const std::string &addr)
  133:         {
  134:             return translateMemoryRead(inst, effectiveMemoryHint, width, addr);
  135:         };
  136: 
  137:         auto genWrite = [&](int width, const std::string &addr, const std::string &val)
  138:         {
  139:             return translateMemoryWrite(inst, effectiveMemoryHint, width, addr, val);
  140:         };
  141: 
  142:         switch (inst.opcode)
  143:         {
  144:         case OPCODE_SPECIAL:
  145:             return m_codeGenerator.translateSpecialInstruction(inst);
  146:         case OPCODE_REGIMM:
  147:             return m_codeGenerator.translateRegimmInstruction(inst);
  148:         case OPCODE_COP0:
  149:             return m_codeGenerator.translateCOP0Instruction(inst);
  150:         case OPCODE_COP1:
  151:             return m_codeGenerator.translateFPUInstruction(inst);
  152:         case OPCODE_COP2:
  153:             return m_codeGenerator.translateVUInstruction(inst);
  154:         case OPCODE_ADDI:
  155:             if (inst.rt == 0)
  156:                 return "// NOP (addi to $zero)";
  157:             return fmt::format(
  158:                 "{{ uint32_t tmp; bool ov; "
  159:                 "ADD32_OV(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), (int32_t){}, tmp, ov); "
  160:                 "if (ov) runtime->SignalException(ctx, EXCEPTION_INTEGER_OVERFLOW); "
  161:                 "else SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int32_t)tmp); }}",
  162:                 inst.rs, inst.simmediate, inst.rt);
  163: 
  164:         case OPCODE_ADDIU:
  165:             if (inst.rt == 0)
  166:                 return "// NOP (addiu $zero, ...)";
  167:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int32_t)ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}));", inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.simmediate);
  168:         case OPCODE_SLTI:
  169:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, ((int64_t)GPR_S64(ctx, {}) < (int64_t)(int32_t){}) ? 1 : 0);", inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.simmediate);
  170:         case OPCODE_SLTIU:
  171:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, ((uint64_t)GPR_U64(ctx, {}) < (uint64_t)(int64_t)(int32_t){}) ? 1 : 0);", inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.simmediate);
  172:         case OPCODE_ANDI:
  173:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, GPR_U64(ctx, {}) & (uint64_t)(uint16_t){});", inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.immediate);
  174:         case OPCODE_ORI:
  175:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, GPR_U64(ctx, {}) | (uint64_t)(uint16_t){});", inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.immediate);
  176:         case OPCODE_XORI:
  177:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, GPR_U64(ctx, {}) ^ (uint64_t)(uint16_t){});", inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.immediate);
  178:         case OPCODE_LUI:
  179:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int32_t)((uint32_t){} << 16));", inst.rt, inst.immediate);
  180:         case OPCODE_LB:
  181:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int8_t){});", inst.rt, genRead(8, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  182:         case OPCODE_LH:
  183:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int16_t){});", inst.rt, genRead(16, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  184:         case OPCODE_LW:
  185:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int32_t){});", inst.rt, genRead(32, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  186:         case OPCODE_LBU:
  187:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_ZE32(ctx, {}, (uint8_t){});", inst.rt, genRead(8, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  188:         case OPCODE_LHU:
  189:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_ZE32(ctx, {}, (uint16_t){});", inst.rt, genRead(16, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  190:         case OPCODE_LWU:
  191:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_ZE32(ctx, {}, {});", inst.rt, genRead(32, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  192:         case OPCODE_SB:
  193:             return genWrite(8, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), fmt::format("(uint8_t)GPR_U32(ctx, {})", inst.rt)) + ";";
  194:         case OPCODE_SH:
  195:             return genWrite(16, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), fmt::format("(uint16_t)GPR_U32(ctx, {})", inst.rt)) + ";";
  196:         case OPCODE_SW:
  197:             return genWrite(32, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), fmt::format("GPR_U32(ctx, {})", inst.rt)) + ";";
  198:         case OPCODE_LQ:
  199:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, {});", inst.rt, genRead(128, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  200:         case OPCODE_SQ:
  201:             return genWrite(128, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), fmt::format("GPR_VEC(ctx, {})", inst.rt)) + ";";
  202:         case OPCODE_LD:
  203:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, {});", inst.rt, genRead(64, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  204:         case OPCODE_SD:
  205:             return genWrite(64, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), fmt::format("GPR_U64(ctx, {})", inst.rt)) + ";";
  206:         case OPCODE_LWC1:
  207:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t bits = {}; float f; std::memcpy(&f, &bits, sizeof(f)); ctx->f[{}] = f; }}", genRead(32, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)), inst.rt);
  208:         case OPCODE_SWC1:
  209:             return fmt::format(
  210:                 "{{ float f = ctx->f[{}]; uint32_t bits; std::memcpy(&bits, &f, sizeof(bits)); {}; }}",
  211:                 inst.rt,
  212:                 genWrite(32, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), "bits"));
  213:         case OPCODE_LDC2:
  214:             return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_vf[{}] = _mm_castsi128_ps({});", inst.rt, genRead(128, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate)));
  215:         case OPCODE_SDC2:
  216:             return genWrite(128, fmt::format("ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {})", inst.rs, inst.simmediate), fmt::format("_mm_castps_si128(ctx->vu0_vf[{}])", inst.rt)) + ";";
  217:         case OPCODE_DADDI:
  218:             return fmt::format(
  219:                 "{{ int64_t src = (int64_t)GPR_S64(ctx, {}); "
  220:                 "int64_t imm = (int64_t)(int32_t){}; "
  221:                 "int64_t res = src + imm; "
  222:                 "if (((src ^ imm) >= 0) && ((src ^ res) < 0)) "
  223:                 "    runtime->SignalException(ctx, EXCEPTION_INTEGER_OVERFLOW); "
  224:                 "else SET_GPR_S64(ctx, {}, res); }}",
  225:                 inst.rs, inst.simmediate, inst.rt);
  226:         case OPCODE_DADDIU:
  227:             return fmt::format(
  228:                 "SET_GPR_S64(ctx, {}, (int64_t)GPR_S64(ctx, {}) + (int64_t)(int32_t){});",
  229:                 inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.simmediate);
  230:         case OPCODE_J:
  231:             return fmt::format("// J 0x{:X} - Handled by branch logic", buildAbsoluteJumpTarget(inst.address, inst.target));
  232:         case OPCODE_JAL:
  233:             return fmt::format("// JAL 0x{:X} - Handled by branch logic", buildAbsoluteJumpTarget(inst.address, inst.target));
  234:         case OPCODE_BEQ:
  235:         case OPCODE_BNE:
  236:         case OPCODE_BLEZ:
  237:         case OPCODE_BGTZ:
  238:         case OPCODE_BEQL:
  239:         case OPCODE_BNEL:
  240:         case OPCODE_BLEZL:
  241:         case OPCODE_BGTZL:
  242:             return fmt::format("// Likely branch instruction at 0x{:X} - Handled by branch logic", inst.address);
  243: 
  244:         case OPCODE_LDL:
  245:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  246:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~7u; "
  247:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 7u; "
  248:                                "uint64_t mem = {}; "
  249:                                "uint32_t shift = (7u - offset) << 3; "
  250:                                "uint64_t keepMask = (shift == 0) ? 0ull : ((1ull << shift) - 1ull); "
  251:                                "SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, (GPR_U64(ctx, {}) & keepMask) | (mem << shift)); }}",
  252:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(64, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, inst.rt);
  253: 
  254:         case OPCODE_LDR:
  255:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  256:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~7u; "
  257:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 7u; "
  258:                                "uint64_t mem = {}; "
  259:                                "uint32_t shift = offset << 3; "
  260:                                "uint64_t keepMask = (offset == 0) ? 0ull : (0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull << ((8u - offset) << 3)); "
  261:                                "SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, (GPR_U64(ctx, {}) & keepMask) | (mem >> shift)); }}",
  262:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(64, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, inst.rt);
  263: 
  264:         case OPCODE_LWL:
  265:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  266:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~3u; "
  267:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 3u; "
  268:                                "uint32_t mem = {}; "
  269:                                "uint32_t shift = (3u - offset) << 3; "
  270:                                "uint32_t keepMask = (shift == 0) ? 0u : ((1u << shift) - 1u); "
  271:                                "uint32_t merged = (GPR_U32(ctx, {}) & keepMask) | (mem << shift); "
  272:                                "SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int32_t)merged); }}",
  273:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(32, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, inst.rt);
  274: 
  275:         case OPCODE_LWR:
  276:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  277:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~3u; "
  278:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 3u; "
  279:                                "uint32_t mem = {}; "
  280:                                "uint32_t shift = offset << 3; "
  281:                                "uint32_t keepMask = (offset == 0) ? 0u : (0xFFFFFFFFu << ((4u - offset) << 3)); "
  282:                                "uint32_t merged32 = (GPR_U32(ctx, {}) & keepMask) | (mem >> shift); "
  283:                                "uint64_t merged64 = (GPR_U64(ctx, {}) & 0xFFFFFFFF00000000ull) | (uint64_t)merged32; "
  284:                                "if (offset == 0) merged64 = (uint64_t)(int64_t)(int32_t)merged32; "
  285:                                "SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, merged64); }}",
  286:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(32, "aligned_addr"),
  287:                                inst.rt, inst.rt, inst.rt);
  288: 
  289:         case OPCODE_SWL:
  290:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  291:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~3u; "
  292:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 3u; "
  293:                                "uint32_t shift = (3u - offset) << 3; "
  294:                                "uint32_t mask = 0xFFFFFFFFu >> shift; "
  295:                                "uint32_t old_data = {}; "
  296:                                "uint32_t val = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); "
  297:                                "uint32_t new_data = (old_data & ~mask) | ((val >> shift) & mask); "
  298:                                "{}; }}",
  299:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(32, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, genWrite(32, "aligned_addr", "new_data"));
  300: 
  301:         case OPCODE_SWR:
  302:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  303:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~3u; "
  304:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 3u; "
  305:                                "uint32_t shift = offset << 3; "
  306:                                "uint32_t mask = 0xFFFFFFFFu << shift; "
  307:                                "uint32_t old_data = {}; "
  308:                                "uint32_t val = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); "
  309:                                "uint32_t new_data = (old_data & ~mask) | ((val << shift) & mask); "
  310:                                "{}; }}",
  311:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(32, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, genWrite(32, "aligned_addr", "new_data"));
  312: 
  313:         case OPCODE_SDL:
  314:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  315:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~7u; "
  316:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 7u; "
  317:                                "uint32_t shift = (7u - offset) << 3; "
  318:                                "uint64_t mask = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull >> shift; "
  319:                                "uint64_t old_data = {}; "
  320:                                "uint64_t val = GPR_U64(ctx, {}); "
  321:                                "uint64_t new_data = (old_data & ~mask) | ((val >> shift) & mask); "
  322:                                "{}; }}",
  323:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(64, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, genWrite(64, "aligned_addr", "new_data"));
  324: 
  325:         case OPCODE_SDR:
  326:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  327:                                "uint32_t aligned_addr = addr & ~7u; "
  328:                                "uint32_t offset = addr & 7u; "
  329:                                "uint32_t shift = offset << 3; "
  330:                                "uint64_t mask = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull << shift; "
  331:                                "uint64_t old_data = {}; "
  332:                                "uint64_t val = GPR_U64(ctx, {}); "
  333:                                "uint64_t new_data = (old_data & ~mask) | ((val << shift) & mask); "
  334:                                "{}; }}",
  335:                                inst.rs, inst.simmediate, genRead(64, "aligned_addr"), inst.rt, genWrite(64, "aligned_addr", "new_data"));
  336:         case OPCODE_CACHE:
  337:             return "// CACHE instruction (ignored)";
  338:         case OPCODE_PREF:
  339:             return "// PREF instruction (ignored)";
  340:         case OPCODE_LL:
  341:             return fmt::format(
  342:                 "{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  343:                 "SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, (int32_t)READ32(addr)); "
  344:                 "ctx->llbit = 1; ctx->lladdr = addr; }}",
  345:                 inst.rs, inst.simmediate, inst.rt);
  346:         case OPCODE_SC:
  347:             return fmt::format(
  348:                 "{{ uint32_t addr = ADD32(GPR_U32(ctx, {}), {}); "
  349:                 "if (ctx->llbit && ctx->lladdr == addr) {{ WRITE32(addr, GPR_U32(ctx, {})); "
  350:                 "SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, 1); }} "
  351:                 "else {{ SET_GPR_S32(ctx, {}, 0); }} "
  352:                 "ctx->llbit = 0; ctx->lladdr = 0; }}",
  353:                 inst.rs, inst.simmediate, inst.rt, inst.rt, inst.rt);
  354:         default:
  355:             return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled opcode: 0x{:X}", inst.opcode));
  356:         }
  357:     }

   24:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
   25:         uint8_t sa = inst.sa;
   26:         switch (function)
   27:         {
   28:         case MMI_MFHI1:
   29:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, ctx->hi1);", rd);

   32:         case MMI_MFLO1:

   36:         case MMI_MULT1:

   41:             return fmt::format("{{ int64_t result = (int64_t)GPR_S32(ctx, {}) * (int64_t)GPR_S32(ctx, {}); ctx->lo1 = (uint64_t)(int64_t)(int32_t)result; ctx->hi1 = (uint64_t)(int64_t)(int32_t)(result >> 32); }}", rs, rt);

   46:             }
   47:             return fmt::format("{{ uint64_t result = (uint64_t)GPR_U32(ctx, {}) * (uint64_t)GPR_U32(ctx, {}); ctx->lo1 = (uint64_t)(int64_t)(int32_t)result; ctx->hi1 = (uint64_t)(int64_t)(int32_t)(result >> 32); }}", rs, rt);

   59:                                "    ctx->lo1 = (dividend < 0) ? 1ull : 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFull; ctx->hi1 = (uint64_t)(int64_t)dividend; "
