    8: 
    9: namespace ps2recomp
   10: {
   11:     std::string CodeGenerator::translateMMI0Instruction(const Instruction &inst)
   12:     {
   13:         uint8_t subfunc = inst.sa;
   14:         uint8_t rs = inst.rs;
   15:         uint8_t rt = inst.rt;
   16:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
   17:         switch (subfunc)
   18:         {
   19:         case MMI0_PADDW:
   20:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PADDW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   21:         case MMI0_PSUBW:
   22:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSUBW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   23:         case MMI0_PCGTW:
   24:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCGTW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   25:         case MMI0_PMAXW:
   26:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMAXW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   27:         case MMI0_PADDH:
   28:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PADDH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   29:         case MMI0_PSUBH:
   30:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSUBH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   31:         case MMI0_PCGTH:
   32:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCGTH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   33:         case MMI0_PMAXH:
   34:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMAXH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   35:         case MMI0_PADDB:
   36:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PADDB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   37:         case MMI0_PSUBB:
   38:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSUBB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   39:         case MMI0_PCGTB:
   40:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCGTB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   41:         case MMI0_PADDSW:
   42:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PADDSW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   43:         case MMI0_PSUBSW:
   44:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSUBSW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   45:         case MMI0_PEXTLW:
   46:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXTLW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   47:         case MMI0_PPACW:
   48:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PPACW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   49:         case MMI0_PADDSH:
   50:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_adds_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   51:         case MMI0_PSUBSH:
   52:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_subs_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   53:         case MMI0_PEXTLH:
   54:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXTLH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   55:         case MMI0_PPACH:
   56:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PPACH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   57:         case MMI0_PADDSB:
   58:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_adds_epi8(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   59:         case MMI0_PSUBSB:
   60:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_subs_epi8(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   61:         case MMI0_PEXTLB:
   62:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXTLB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   63:         case MMI0_PPACB:
   64:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PPACB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   65:         case MMI0_PEXT5:
   66:             return translatePEXT5(inst);
   67:         case MMI0_PPAC5:
   68:             return translatePPAC5(inst);
   69:         default:
   70:             return emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled MMI0 instruction: function 0x{:X}", subfunc));
   71:         }
   72:     }
   73: 
   74: 
   75:     std::string CodeGenerator::translateMMI1Instruction(const Instruction &inst)
   76:     {
   77:         uint8_t subfunc = inst.sa;
   78:         uint8_t rs = inst.rs;
   79:         uint8_t rt = inst.rt;
   80:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
   81:         switch (subfunc)
   82:         {
   83:         case MMI1_PABSW:
   84:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PABSW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs);
   85:         case MMI1_PCEQW:
   86:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCEQW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   87:         case MMI1_PMINW:
   88:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMINW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   89:         case MMI1_PADSBH:
   90:             return translatePADSBH(inst);
   91:         case MMI1_PABSH:
   92:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PABSH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs);
   93:         case MMI1_PCEQH:
   94:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCEQH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   95:         case MMI1_PMINH:
   96:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMINH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   97:         case MMI1_PCEQB:
   98:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCEQB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
   99:         case MMI1_PADDUW:
  100:             return fmt::format(
  101:                 "SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, ps2_paddu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  102:         case MMI1_PSUBUW:
  103:             return fmt::format(
  104:                 "SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, ps2_psubu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  105:         case MMI1_PEXTUW:
  106:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXTUW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  107:         case MMI1_PADDUH:
  108:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_add_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  109:         case MMI1_PSUBUH:
  110:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_sub_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  111:         case MMI1_PEXTUH:
  112:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXTUH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  113:         case MMI1_PADDUB:
  114:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_adds_epu8(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  115:         case MMI1_PSUBUB:
  116:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_subs_epu8(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  117:         case MMI1_PEXTUB:
  118:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXTUB(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  119:         case MMI1_QFSRV:
  120:             return translateQFSRV(inst);
  121:         default:
  122:             return emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled MMI1 instruction: function 0x{:X}", subfunc));
  123:         }
  124:     }

  126: 
  127:     std::string CodeGenerator::translateMMI2Instruction(const Instruction &inst)
  128:     {
  129:         uint8_t subfunc = inst.sa;
  130:         uint8_t rs = inst.rs;
  131:         uint8_t rt = inst.rt;
  132:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
  133:         switch (subfunc)
  134:         {
  135:         case MMI2_PMADDW:
  136:             return translatePMADDW(inst);
  137:         case MMI2_PSLLVW:
  138:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSLLVW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  139:         case MMI2_PSRLVW:
  140:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSRLVW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  141:         case MMI2_PMSUBW:
  142:             return translatePMSUBW(inst);
  143:         case MMI2_PMFHI:
  144:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, ctx->hi);", rd);
  145:         case MMI2_PMFLO:
  146:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, ctx->lo);", rd);
  147:         case MMI2_PINTH:
  148:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PINTH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  149:         case MMI2_PMULTW:
  150:             return translatePMULTW(inst);
  151:         case MMI2_PDIVW:
  152:             return translatePDIVW(inst);
  153:         case MMI2_PCPYLD:
  154:             return translatePCPYLD(inst);
  155:         case MMI2_PAND:
  156:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PAND(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  157:         case MMI2_PXOR:
  158:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PXOR(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  159:         case MMI2_PMADDH:
  160:             return translatePMADDH(inst);
  161:         case MMI2_PHMADH:
  162:             return translatePHMADH(inst);
  163:         case MMI2_PMSUBH:
  164:             return translatePMSUBH(inst);
  165:         case MMI2_PHMSBH:
  166:             return translatePHMSBH(inst);
  167:         case MMI2_PEXEH:
  168:             return translatePEXEH(inst);
  169:         case MMI2_PREVH:
  170:             return translatePREVH(inst);
  171:         case MMI2_PMULTH:
  172:             return translatePMULTH(inst);
  173:         case MMI2_PDIVBW:
  174:             return translatePDIVBW(inst);
  175:         case MMI2_PEXEW:
  176:             return translatePEXEW(inst);
  177:         case MMI2_PROT3W:
  178:             return translatePROT3W(inst);
  179:         default:
  180:             return emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled MMI2 instruction: function 0x{:X}", subfunc));
  181:         }
  182:     }

  184: 
  185:     std::string CodeGenerator::translateMMI3Instruction(const Instruction &inst)
  186:     {
  187:         uint8_t subfunc = inst.sa;
  188:         uint8_t rs = inst.rs;
  189:         uint8_t rt = inst.rt;
  190:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
  191:         switch (subfunc)
  192:         {
  193:         case MMI3_PMADDUW:
  194:             return translatePMADDUW(inst);
  195:         case MMI3_PSRAVW:
  196:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PSRAVW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  197:         case MMI3_PMTHI:
  198:             return translatePMTHI(inst);
  199:         case MMI3_PMTLO:
  200:             return translatePMTLO(inst);
  201:         case MMI3_PINTEH:
  202:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PINTEH(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  203:         case MMI3_PMULTUW:
  204:             return translatePMULTUW(inst);
  205:         case MMI3_PDIVUW:
  206:             return translatePDIVUW(inst);
  207:         case MMI3_PCPYUD:
  208:             return translatePCPYUD(inst);
  209:         case MMI3_POR:
  210:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_POR(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  211:         case MMI3_PNOR:
  212:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PNOR(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));", rd, rs, rt);
  213:         case MMI3_PEXCH:
  214:             return translatePEXCH(inst);
  215:         case MMI3_PCPYH:
  216:             return translatePCPYH(inst);
  217:         case MMI3_PEXCW:
  218:             return translatePEXCW(inst);
  219:         default:
  220:             return emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled MMI3 instruction: function 0x{:X}", subfunc));
  221:         }
  222:     }
  223: 
  224: 
  225:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMFHLInstruction(const Instruction &inst)
  226:     {
  227:         uint8_t subfunc = inst.sa;
  228:         switch (subfunc)
  229:         {
  230:         case PMFHL_LW:
  231:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMFHL_LW(ctx));", inst.rd);
  232:         case PMFHL_UW:
  233:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMFHL_UW(ctx));", inst.rd);
  234:         case PMFHL_SLW:
  235:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMFHL_SLW(ctx));", inst.rd);
  236:         case PMFHL_LH:
  237:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMFHL_LH(ctx));", inst.rd);
  238:         case PMFHL_SH:
  239:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PMFHL_SH(ctx));", inst.rd);
  240:         default:
  241:             return emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled PMFHL instruction: function 0x{:X}", subfunc));
  242:         }
  243:     }
  244: 
  245: 
  246:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMTHLInstruction(const Instruction &inst)
  247:     {
  248:         uint8_t subfunc = inst.sa;
  249:         switch (subfunc)
  250:         {
  251:         case PMFHL_LW:
  252:             return fmt::format("PS2_PMTHL_LW(ctx, GPR_VEC(ctx, {}));", inst.rs);
  253:         default:
  254:             return emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled PMTHL instruction: function 0x{:X}", subfunc));
  255:         }
  256:     }
  257: 
  258: 
  259:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePEXT5(const Instruction &inst)
  260:     {
  261:         return fmt::format(
  262:             "{{ __m128i rt = GPR_VEC(ctx, {}); \n"
  263:             "   __m128i m1 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x0000001F); \n"
  264:             "   __m128i m2 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x000003E0); \n"
  265:             "   __m128i m3 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x00007C00); \n"
  266:             "   __m128i m4 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x00008000); \n"
  267:             "   __m128i a1 = _mm_slli_epi32(_mm_and_si128(rt, m1), 3); \n"
  268:             "   __m128i a2 = _mm_slli_epi32(_mm_and_si128(rt, m2), 6); \n"
  269:             "   __m128i a3 = _mm_slli_epi32(_mm_and_si128(rt, m3), 9); \n"
  270:             "   __m128i a4 = _mm_slli_epi32(_mm_and_si128(rt, m4), 16); \n"
  271:             "   SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_or_si128(_mm_or_si128(a1, a2), _mm_or_si128(a3, a4))); }}",
  272:             inst.rt, inst.rd);
  273:     }
  274: 
  275: 
  276:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePPAC5(const Instruction &inst)
  277:     {
  278:         return fmt::format(
  279:             "{{ __m128i rt = GPR_VEC(ctx, {}); \n"
  280:             "   __m128i m1 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x0000001F); \n"
  281:             "   __m128i m2 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x000003E0); \n"
  282:             "   __m128i m3 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x00007C00); \n"
  283:             "   __m128i m4 = _mm_set1_epi32(0x00008000); \n"
  284:             "   __m128i a1 = _mm_and_si128(_mm_srli_epi32(rt, 3), m1); \n"
  285:             "   __m128i a2 = _mm_and_si128(_mm_srli_epi32(rt, 6), m2); \n"
  286:             "   __m128i a3 = _mm_and_si128(_mm_srli_epi32(rt, 9), m3); \n"
  287:             "   __m128i a4 = _mm_and_si128(_mm_srli_epi32(rt, 16), m4); \n"
  288:             "   SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_or_si128(_mm_or_si128(a1, a2), _mm_or_si128(a3, a4))); }}",
  289:             inst.rt, inst.rd);
  290:     }
  291: 
  292: 
  293:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePADSBH(const Instruction &inst)
  294:     {
  295:         return fmt::format(
  296:             "{{ __m128i rs = GPR_VEC(ctx, {}); __m128i rt = GPR_VEC(ctx, {}); \n"
  297:             "   __m128i sub = _mm_sub_epi16(rs, rt); \n"
  298:             "   __m128i add = _mm_add_epi16(rs, rt); \n"
  299:             "   SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_unpacklo_epi64(sub, _mm_unpackhi_epi64(add, add))); }}",
  300:             inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  301:     }
  302: 
  303: 
  304:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMADDW(const Instruction &inst)
  305:     {
  306:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i p01 = _mm_mul_epu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  307:                            "   __m128i p23 = _mm_mul_epu32(_mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8), _mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8)); \n"
  308:                            "   uint64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  309:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p01); \n"
  310:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p01, 8)); \n"
  311:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p23); \n"
  312:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p23, 8)); \n"
  313:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  314:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  315:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  316:     }
  317: 
  318: 
  319:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMSUBW(const Instruction &inst)
  320:     {
  321:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i p01 = _mm_mul_epu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  322:                            "   __m128i p23 = _mm_mul_epu32(_mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8), _mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8)); \n"
  323:                            "   uint64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  324:                            "   acc -= _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p01); \n"
  325:                            "   acc -= _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p01, 8)); \n"
  326:                            "   acc -= _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p23); \n"
  327:                            "   acc -= _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p23, 8)); \n"
  328:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  329:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  330:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  331:     }
  332: 
  333: 
  334:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMULTW(const Instruction &inst)
  335:     {
  336:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i p01 = _mm_mul_epu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  337:                            "   __m128i p23 = _mm_mul_epu32(_mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8), _mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8)); \n"
  338:                            "   uint64_t acc = 0; \n"
  339:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p01); \n"
  340:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p01, 8)); \n"
  341:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p23); \n"
  342:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p23, 8)); \n"
  343:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  344:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  345:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  346:     }
  347: 
  348: 
  349:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMADDUW(const Instruction &inst)
  350:     {
  351:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i p01 = _mm_mul_epu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  352:                            "   __m128i p23 = _mm_mul_epu32(_mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8), _mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8)); \n"
  353:                            "   uint64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  354:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p01); \n"
  355:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p01, 8)); \n"
  356:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p23); \n"
  357:                            "   acc += _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p23, 8)); \n"
  358:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  359:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  360:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  361:     }
  362: 
  363: 
  364:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePDIVW(const Instruction &inst)
  365:     {
  366:         // Only divides the first word element rs[0] / rt[0]
  367:         return fmt::format("{{ int32_t rs0 = GPR_S32(ctx, {}); int32_t rt0 = GPR_S32(ctx, {}); \n"
  368:                            "   if (rt0 != 0) {{ \n"
  369:                            "       if (rt0 == -1 && rs0 == INT32_MIN) {{ ctx->lo = (uint32_t)INT32_MIN; ctx->hi = 0; }} \n"
  370:                            "       else {{ ctx->lo = (uint32_t)(rs0 / rt0); ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(rs0 % rt0); }} \n"
  371:                            "   }} else {{ ctx->lo = (rs0 < 0) ? 1 : -1; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)rs0; }} \n"
  372:                            "   SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->lo); }}",
  373:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  374:     }
  375: 
  376: 
  377:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePCPYLD(const Instruction &inst)
  378:     {
  379:         // PCPYLD uses rs as the upper source and rt as the lower source.
  380:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PCPYLD(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));",
  381:                            inst.rd, inst.rs, inst.rt);
  382:     }
  383: 
  384: 
  385:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMADDH(const Instruction &inst)
  386:     {
  387:         // Parallel multiply add halfword -> results to HI/LO and rd
  388:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i prod = _mm_madd_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n" // Packed multiply and add adjacent pairs
  389:                            "   int32_t p0 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(prod); \n"
  390:                            "   int32_t p1 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 4)); \n"
  391:                            "   int32_t p2 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 8)); \n"
  392:                            "   int32_t p3 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 12)); \n"
  393:                            "   int64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  394:                            "   acc += (int64_t)p0 + (int64_t)p1 + (int64_t)p2 + (int64_t)p3; \n"
  395:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  396:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  397:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  398:     }
  399: 
  400: 
  401:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePHMADH(const Instruction &inst)
  402:     {
  403:         // Parallel Horizontal Multiply Add Halfword -> results to HI/LO and rd
  404:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i evens = _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,0,2,0)); \n" // Select even halfwords
  405:                            "   __m128i odds  = _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(3,1,3,1)); \n" // Select odd halfwords
  406:                            "   __m128i prod_ev = _mm_mullo_epi16(evens, _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,0,2,0))); \n"
  407:                            "   __m128i prod_od = _mm_mullo_epi16(odds,  _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(3,1,3,1))); \n"
  408:                            "   __m128i sum_pairs = _mm_add_epi16(prod_ev, prod_od); \n"                            // Add rs[0]*rt[0] + rs[1]*rt[1], etc.
  409:                            "   int32_t h0 = _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 0) + _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 1); \n" // Horizontal add within low 32b
  410:                            "   int32_t h1 = _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 2) + _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 3); \n" // Horizontal add within next 32b
  411:                            "   int32_t h2 = _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 4) + _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 5); \n"
  412:                            "   int32_t h3 = _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 6) + _mm_extract_epi16(sum_pairs, 7); \n"
  413:                            "   int64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  414:                            "   acc += (int64_t)h0 + (int64_t)h1 + (int64_t)h2 + (int64_t)h3; \n"
  415:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  416:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  417:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  418:     }
  419: 
  420: 
  421:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMSUBH(const Instruction &inst)
  422:     {
  423:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i prod = _mm_madd_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  424:                            "   int32_t p0 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(prod); \n"
  425:                            "   int32_t p1 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 4)); \n"
  426:                            "   int32_t p2 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 8)); \n"
  427:                            "   int32_t p3 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 12)); \n"
  428:                            "   int64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  429:                            "   acc -= (int64_t)p0 + (int64_t)p1 + (int64_t)p2 + (int64_t)p3; \n"
  430:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  431:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  432:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  433:     }
  434: 
  435: 
  436:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePHMSBH(const Instruction &inst)
  437:     {
  438:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i evens = _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,0,2,0)); \n"
  439:                            "   __m128i odds  = _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(3,1,3,1)); \n"
  440:                            "   __m128i prod_ev = _mm_mullo_epi16(evens, _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,0,2,0))); \n"
  441:                            "   __m128i prod_od = _mm_mullo_epi16(odds,  _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(3,1,3,1))); \n"
  442:                            "   __m128i sub_pairs = _mm_sub_epi16(prod_od, prod_ev); \n"
  443:                            "   int32_t h0 = _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 0) + _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 1); \n"
  444:                            "   int32_t h1 = _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 2) + _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 3); \n"
  445:                            "   int32_t h2 = _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 4) + _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 5); \n"
  446:                            "   int32_t h3 = _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 6) + _mm_extract_epi16(sub_pairs, 7); \n"
  447:                            "   int64_t acc = Ps2HiLoToU64(ctx->hi, ctx->lo); \n"
  448:                            "   acc += (int64_t)h0 + (int64_t)h1 + (int64_t)h2 + (int64_t)h3; \n"
  449:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)acc; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(acc >> 32); \n"
  450:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, acc); }}",
  451:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  452:     }
  453: 
  454: 
  455:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePEXEH(const Instruction &inst)
  456:     {
  457:         // Swaps halfwords 1<->3 and 5<->7 within the 128-bit register
  458:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_shufflelo_epi16(_mm_shufflehi_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,3,0,1)), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,3,0,1)));",
  459:                            inst.rd, inst.rt);
  460:     }
  461: 
  462: 
  463:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePREVH(const Instruction &inst)
  464:     {
  465:         // Reverses the order of the 8 halfwords
  466:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i mask = _mm_setr_epi8(14,15, 12,13, 10,11, 8,9, 6,7, 4,5, 2,3, 0,1); "
  467:                            "SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_SHUFFLE_EPI8(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), mask)); }}",
  468:                            inst.rd, inst.rt);
  469:     }
  470: 
  471: 
  472:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMULTH(const Instruction &inst)
  473:     {
  474:         // Parallel multiply halfword, results sum to HI/LO and rd
  475:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i prod = _mm_madd_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  476:                            "   int32_t p0 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(prod); \n"
  477:                            "   int32_t p1 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 4)); \n"
  478:                            "   int32_t p2 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 8)); \n"
  479:                            "   int32_t p3 = _mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_srli_si128(prod, 12)); \n"
  480:                            "   int64_t result = (int64_t)p0 + (int64_t)p1 + (int64_t)p2 + (int64_t)p3; \n"
  481:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)result; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(result >> 32); \n"
  482:                            "   SET_GPR_U64(ctx, {}, result); }}",
  483:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  484:     }
  485: 
  486: 
  487:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePDIVBW(const Instruction &inst)
  488:     {
  489:         return fmt::format(
  490:             "{{\n"
  491:             "    __m128i rsVec = GPR_VEC(ctx, {});\n"
  492:             "    __m128i rtVec = GPR_VEC(ctx, {});\n"
  493:             "    alignas(16) int32_t rsWords[4];\n"
  494:             "    alignas(16) int32_t rtWords[4];\n"
  495:             "    _mm_store_si128((__m128i*)rsWords, rsVec);\n"
  496:             "    _mm_store_si128((__m128i*)rtWords, rtVec);\n"
  497:             "    int32_t div = rtWords[0];\n"
  498:             "    int32_t q0 = 0, q1 = 0, q2 = 0, q3 = 0;\n"
  499:             "    if (div != 0) {{\n"
  500:             "        q0 = rsWords[0] / div; ctx->lo = (uint32_t)q0; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(rsWords[0] % div);\n"
  501:             "        q1 = rsWords[1] / div;\n"
  502:             "        q2 = rsWords[2] / div;\n"
  503:             "        q3 = rsWords[3] / div;\n"
  504:             "    }} else {{\n"
  505:             "        ctx->lo = (rsWords[0] < 0) ? 1 : -1;\n"
  506:             "        ctx->hi = (uint32_t)rsWords[0];\n"
  507:             "    }}\n"
  508:             "    SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_set_epi32(q3, q2, q1, q0));\n"
  509:             "}}",
  510:             inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  511:     }
  512: 
  513: 
  514:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePEXEW(const Instruction &inst)
  515:     {
  516:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_PEXEW(GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));",
  517:                            inst.rd, inst.rt);
  518:     }
  519: 
  520: 
  521:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePROT3W(const Instruction &inst)
  522:     {
  523:         // Rotates words left by 3: [d,c,b,a] -> [a,d,c,b]
  524:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(0,3,2,1)));",
  525:                            inst.rd, inst.rt);
  526:     }
  527: 
  528: 
  529:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMULTUW(const Instruction &inst)
  530:     {
  531:         // Parallel multiply unsigned word -> results to HI/LO and rd (lower 32 bits)
  532:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i p01 = _mm_mul_epu32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})); \n"
  533:                            "   __m128i p23 = _mm_mul_epu32(_mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8), _mm_srli_si128(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), 8)); \n"
  534:                            "   uint64_t res0 = _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p01); uint64_t res1 = _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p01, 8)); \n"
  535:                            "   uint64_t res2 = _mm_cvtsi128_si64(p23); uint64_t res3 = _mm_cvtsi128_si64(_mm_srli_si128(p23, 8)); \n"
  536:                            "   ctx->lo = (uint32_t)res0; ctx->hi = (uint32_t)(res0 >> 32); \n" // HI/LO from first product only
  537:                            "   SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_set_epi32((uint32_t)res3, (uint32_t)res2, (uint32_t)res1, (uint32_t)res0)); }}",
  538:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  539:     }
  540: 
  541: 
  542:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePDIVUW(const Instruction &inst)
  543:     {
  544:         // Parallel divide unsigned word (only first element) -> results to HI/LO and rd (quotient)
  545:         return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t rs0 = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); uint32_t rt0 = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); \n"
  546:                            "   if (rt0 != 0) {{ ctx->lo = rs0 / rt0; ctx->hi = rs0 % rt0; }} \n"
  547:                            "   else {{ ctx->lo = 0xFFFFFFFF; ctx->hi = rs0; }} \n" // Div by zero behavior
  548:                            "   SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->lo); }}",
  549:                            inst.rs, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  550:     }
  551: 
  552: 
  553:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePCPYUD(const Instruction &inst)
  554:     {
  555:         // Copies upper 64 of rs to lower 64 of rd, upper 64 of rt to upper 64 of rd
  556:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_unpackhi_epi64(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {})));",
  557:                            inst.rd, inst.rs, inst.rt); // Order matters
  558:     }
  559: 
  560: 
  561:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePEXCH(const Instruction &inst)
  562:     {
  563:         // Parallel Exchange Center Halfword (same as MMI2 PEXEH)
  564:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_shufflelo_epi16(_mm_shufflehi_epi16(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,3,0,1)), _MM_SHUFFLE(2,3,0,1)));",
  565:                            inst.rd, inst.rt);
  566:     }
  567: 
  568: 
  569:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePCPYH(const Instruction &inst)
  570:     {
  571:         // Parallel Copy Halfword (Broadcast lower 16 bits of each 64-bit half)
  572:         return fmt::format("{{ __m128i src = GPR_VEC(ctx, {}); uint16_t l = _mm_extract_epi16(src, 0); uint16_t h = _mm_extract_epi16(src, 4); \n"
  573:                            "   SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_set_epi16(h,h,h,h, l,l,l,l)); }}",
  574:                            inst.rt, inst.rd);
  575:     }
  576: 
  577: 
  578:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePEXCW(const Instruction &inst)
  579:     {
  580:         // Parallel Exchange Center Word (Swaps words 0<>2, 1<>3)
  581:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_shuffle_epi32(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), _MM_SHUFFLE(1,0,3,2)));",
  582:                            inst.rd, inst.rt);
  583:     }
  584: 
  585: 
  586:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMTHI(const Instruction &inst)
  587:     {
  588:         return fmt::format("ctx->hi = GPR_U32(ctx, {});", inst.rs); // PMTHI uses standard HI/LO
  589:     }
  590: 
  591: 
  592:     std::string CodeGenerator::translatePMTLO(const Instruction &inst)
  593:     {
  594:         return fmt::format("ctx->lo = GPR_U32(ctx, {});", inst.rs); // PMTLO uses standard HI/LO
  595:     }
  596: 
  597: 
  598:     std::string CodeGenerator::translateQFSRV(const Instruction &inst)
  599:     {
  600:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
  601:         uint8_t rs = inst.rs;
  602:         uint8_t rt = inst.rt;
  603:         // QFSRV semantics are centralized in runtime macro helpers.
  604:         return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, PS2_QFSRV(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), GPR_VEC(ctx, {}), ctx->sa & 0x7F));",
  605:                            rd, rs, rt);
  606:     }
  607: 
  608: }

    1: #include "ps2recomp/Translators/vu_translator.h"

   12: namespace ps2recomp
   13: {
   14:     VuTranslator::VuTranslator(CodeGenerator &codeGenerator)
   15:         : m_codeGenerator(codeGenerator)
   16:     {
   17:     }
   18: 
   19:     std::string VuTranslator::translate(const Instruction &inst)
   20:     {
   21:         uint8_t format = inst.rs; // Use parsed rs field for COP2 format
   22:         uint8_t rt = inst.rt;
   23:         uint8_t rd = inst.rd;
   24:         uint8_t sa = inst.sa;
   25: 
   26:         switch (format)
   27:         {
   28:         case COP2_QMFC2:
   29:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_VEC(ctx, {}, _mm_castps_si128(ctx->vu0_vf[{}]));", rt, rd);
   30:         case COP2_CFC2:
   31:         {
   32:             // CFC2/CTC2 use the same 5-bit register field for VI0..VI15 and the VU special control registers.
   33:             if (rd < 16)
   34:             {
   35:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, static_cast<uint32_t>(ctx->vi[{}]));", rt, rd);
   36:             }
   37: 
   38:             switch (rd)
   39:             {
   40:             case VU0_CR_STATUS:
   41:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_status);", rt);
   42:             case VU0_CR_MAC:
   43:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_mac_flags);", rt);
   44:             case VU0_CR_CLIP:
   45:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_clip_flags & 0x00FFFFFFu);", rt);
   46:             case VU0_CR_R:
   47:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, static_cast<uint32_t>(_mm_cvtsi128_si32(_mm_castps_si128(ctx->vu0_r))));", rt);
   48:             case VU0_CR_I:
   49:                 return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t bits; std::memcpy(&bits, &ctx->vu0_i, sizeof(bits)); SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, bits); }}", rt);
   50:             case VU0_CR_Q:
   51:                 return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t bits; std::memcpy(&bits, &ctx->vu0_q, sizeof(bits)); SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, bits); }}", rt);
   52:             case VU0_CR_TPC:
   53:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_tpc >> 3);", rt);
   54:             case VU0_CR_CMSAR0:
   55:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_cmsar0);", rt);
   56:             case VU0_CR_FBRST:
   57:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_fbrst);", rt);
   58:             case VU0_CR_VPU_STAT:
   59:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_vpu_stat);", rt);
   60:             case VU0_CR_CMSAR1:
   61:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->vu0_cmsar1);", rt);
   62:             default:
   63:                 return fmt::format("// Unimplemented CFC2 VU control register: {}", rd);
   64:             }
   65:         }
   66:         case COP2_QMTC2:
   67:             return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_vf[{}] = _mm_castsi128_ps(GPR_VEC(ctx, {}));", rd, rt);
   68:         case COP2_CTC2:
   69:         {
   70:             if (rd < 16)
   71:             {
   72:                 if (rd == 0)
   73:                 {
   74:                     return "// CTC2 write to VI0 ignored";
   75:                 }
   76:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vi[{}] = static_cast<uint16_t>(GPR_U32(ctx, {}));", rd, rt);
   77:             }
   78: 
   79:             switch (rd)
   80:             {
   81:             case VU0_CR_STATUS:
   82:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_status = static_cast<uint16_t>(GPR_U32(ctx, {}) & 0xFFFFu);", rt);
   83:             case VU0_CR_MAC:
   84:             case VU0_CR_TPC:
   85:             case VU0_CR_VPU_STAT:
   86:                 return fmt::format("// CTC2 write to read-only VU control register {} ignored", rd);
   87:             case VU0_CR_CLIP:
   88:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_clip_flags = GPR_U32(ctx, {}) & 0x00FFFFFFu;", rt);
   89:             case VU0_CR_R:
   90:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_r = _mm_castsi128_ps(_mm_set1_epi32(static_cast<int32_t>(GPR_U32(ctx, {}))));", rt);
   91:             case VU0_CR_I:
   92:                 return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t tmp = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); std::memcpy(&ctx->vu0_i, &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", rt);
   93:             case VU0_CR_Q:
   94:                 return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t tmp = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); std::memcpy(&ctx->vu0_q, &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", rt);
   95:             case VU0_CR_CMSAR0:
   96:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_cmsar0 = GPR_U32(ctx, {});", rt);
   97:             case VU0_CR_FBRST:
   98:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_fbrst = GPR_U32(ctx, {}) & 0x00000C0Cu;", rt);
   99:             case VU0_CR_CMSAR1:
  100:                 return fmt::format("ctx->vu0_cmsar1 = GPR_U32(ctx, {});", rt);
  101:             default:
  102:                 return fmt::format("// Unimplemented CTC2 VU control register: {}", rd);
  103:             }
  104:         }
  105:         case COP2_BC:
  106:             return fmt::format("// BC2 (Condition: 0x{:X}) - Handled by branch logic", rt);
  107:         case COP2_CO:
  108:         case COP2_CO + 1:
  109:         case COP2_CO + 2:
  110:         case COP2_CO + 3:
  111:         case COP2_CO + 4:
  112:         case COP2_CO + 5:
  113:         case COP2_CO + 6:
  114:         case COP2_CO + 7:
  115:         case COP2_CO + 8:
  116:         case COP2_CO + 9:
  117:         case COP2_CO + 10:
  118:         case COP2_CO + 11:
  119:         case COP2_CO + 12:
  120:         case COP2_CO + 13:
  121:         case COP2_CO + 14:
  122:         case COP2_CO + 15:
  123:         {
  124:             const uint8_t special1_func = static_cast<uint8_t>(inst.function & 0x3F);
  125:             if (special1_func >= 0x3C) // Special2 Table
  126:             {
  127:                 const uint8_t vu_func = static_cast<uint8_t>((((inst.raw >> 6) & 0x1F) << 2) | (inst.raw & 0x3));
  128:                 switch (vu_func)
  129:                 {
  130:                 case VU0_S2_VADDAx:
  131:                 case VU0_S2_VADDAy:
  132:                 case VU0_S2_VADDAz:
  133:                 case VU0_S2_VADDAw:
  134:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADDA_Field(inst);
  135:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBAx:
  136:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBAy:
  137:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBAz:
  138:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBAw:
  139:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUBA_Field(inst);
  140:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDAx:
  141:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDAy:
  142:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDAz:
  143:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDAw:
  144:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADDA_Field(inst);
  145:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBAx:
  146:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBAy:
  147:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBAz:
  148:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBAw:
  149:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUBA_Field(inst);
  150:                 case VU0_S2_VMULAx:
  151:                 case VU0_S2_VMULAy:
  152:                 case VU0_S2_VMULAz:
  153:                 case VU0_S2_VMULAw:
  154:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMULA_Field(inst);
  155:                 case VU0_S2_VADDA:
  156:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADDA(inst);
  157:                 case VU0_S2_VADDAq:
  158:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADDAq(inst);
  159:                 case VU0_S2_VADDAi:
  160:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADDAi(inst);
  161:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDA:
  162:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADDA(inst);
  163:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDAq:
  164:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADDAq(inst);
  165:                 case VU0_S2_VMADDAi:
  166:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADDAi(inst);
  167:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBA:
  168:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUBA(inst);
  169:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBAq:
  170:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUBAq(inst);
  171:                 case VU0_S2_VSUBAi:
  172:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUBAi(inst);
  173:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBA:
  174:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUBA(inst);
  175:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBAq:
  176:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUBAq(inst);
  177:                 case VU0_S2_VMSUBAi:
  178:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUBAi(inst);
  179:                 case VU0_S2_VMULA:
  180:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMULA(inst);
  181:                 case VU0_S2_VMULAq:
  182:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMULAq(inst);
  183:                 case VU0_S2_VMULAi:
  184:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMULAi(inst);
  185:                 case VU0_S2_VOPMULA:
  186:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VOPMULA(inst);
  187:                 case VU0_S2_VITOF0:
  188:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VITOF(inst, 0);
  189:                 case VU0_S2_VITOF4:
  190:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VITOF(inst, 4);
  191:                 case VU0_S2_VITOF12:
  192:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VITOF(inst, 12);
  193:                 case VU0_S2_VITOF15:
  194:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VITOF(inst, 15);
  195:                 case VU0_S2_VFTOI0:
  196:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VFTOI(inst, 0);
  197:                 case VU0_S2_VFTOI4:
  198:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VFTOI(inst, 4);
  199:                 case VU0_S2_VFTOI12:
  200:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VFTOI(inst, 12);
  201:                 case VU0_S2_VFTOI15:
  202:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VFTOI(inst, 15);
  203:                 case VU0_S2_VLQI:
  204:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VLQI(inst);
  205:                 case VU0_S2_VSQI:
  206:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSQI(inst);
  207:                 case VU0_S2_VLQD:
  208:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VLQD(inst);
  209:                 case VU0_S2_VSQD:
  210:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSQD(inst);
  211:                 case VU0_S2_VDIV:
  212:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VDIV(inst);
  213:                 case VU0_S2_VSQRT:
  214:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSQRT(inst);
  215:                 case VU0_S2_VRSQRT:
  216:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VRSQRT(inst);
  217:                 case VU0_S2_VWAITQ:
  218:                     return fmt::format("// VWAITQ (Q already resolved in this runtime)");
  219:                 case VU0_S2_VMTIR:
  220:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMTIR(inst);
  221:                 case VU0_S2_VMFIR:
  222:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMFIR(inst);
  223:                 case VU0_S2_VILWR:
  224:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VILWR(inst);
  225:                 case VU0_S2_VISWR:
  226:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VISWR(inst);
  227:                 case VU0_S2_VABS:
  228:                 {
  229:                     uint8_t dest_mask = inst.vectorInfo.vectorField;
  230:                     return fmt::format("{{ __m128 res = _mm_and_ps(ctx->vu0_vf[{}], _mm_castsi128_ps(_mm_set1_epi32(0x7FFFFFFF))); "
  231:                                        "__m128i mask = _mm_set_epi32({}, {}, {}, {}); "
  232:                                        "ctx->vu0_vf[{}] = _mm_blendv_ps(ctx->vu0_vf[{}], res, _mm_castsi128_ps(mask)); }}",
  233:                                        inst.rd,
  234:                                        (dest_mask & 0x1) ? -1 : 0, (dest_mask & 0x2) ? -1 : 0,
  235:                                        (dest_mask & 0x4) ? -1 : 0, (dest_mask & 0x8) ? -1 : 0,
  236:                                        inst.rt, inst.rt);
  237:                 }
  238:                 case VU0_S2_VMOVE:
  239:                 {
  240:                     uint8_t dest_mask = inst.vectorInfo.vectorField;
  241:                     return fmt::format(
  242:                         "{{ __m128i mask = _mm_set_epi32({}, {}, {}, {}); "
  243:                         "ctx->vu0_vf[{}] = _mm_blendv_ps(ctx->vu0_vf[{}], ctx->vu0_vf[{}], _mm_castsi128_ps(mask)); }}",
  244:                         (dest_mask & 0x1) ? -1 : 0,
  245:                         (dest_mask & 0x2) ? -1 : 0,
  246:                         (dest_mask & 0x4) ? -1 : 0,
  247:                         (dest_mask & 0x8) ? -1 : 0,
  248:                         inst.rt, inst.rt, inst.rd);
  249:                 }
  250:                 case VU0_S2_VMR32:
  251:                 {
  252:                     uint8_t dest_mask = inst.vectorInfo.vectorField;
  253:                     return fmt::format(
  254:                         "{{ __m128 res = _mm_shuffle_ps(ctx->vu0_vf[{}], ctx->vu0_vf[{}], _MM_SHUFFLE(0,3,2,1)); "
  255:                         "__m128i mask = _mm_set_epi32({}, {}, {}, {}); "
  256:                         "ctx->vu0_vf[{}] = _mm_blendv_ps(ctx->vu0_vf[{}], res, _mm_castsi128_ps(mask)); }}",
  257:                         inst.rd, inst.rd,
  258:                         (dest_mask & 0x1) ? -1 : 0,
  259:                         (dest_mask & 0x2) ? -1 : 0,
  260:                         (dest_mask & 0x4) ? -1 : 0,
  261:                         (dest_mask & 0x8) ? -1 : 0,
  262:                         inst.rt, inst.rt);
  263:                 }
  264:                 case VU0_S2_VCLIPw:
  265:                 {
  266:                     uint8_t field = inst.function & 0x3;
  267:                     std::string shuffle_pattern = fmt::format("_MM_SHUFFLE({},{},{},{})", field, field, field, field);
  268: 
  269:                     return fmt::format(
  270:                         "{{ __m128 fs = ctx->vu0_vf[{}]; "
  271:                         "__m128 ft = _mm_shuffle_ps(ctx->vu0_vf[{}], ctx->vu0_vf[{}], {}); "
  272:                         "__m128 neg_ft = _mm_xor_ps(ft, _mm_castsi128_ps(_mm_set1_epi32(0x80000000))); "
  273:                         "__m128 gt = _mm_cmpgt_ps(fs, ft); "
  274:                         "__m128 lt = _mm_cmplt_ps(fs, neg_ft); "
  275:                         "uint32_t gt_mask = (uint32_t)_mm_movemask_ps(gt); "
  276:                         "uint32_t lt_mask = (uint32_t)_mm_movemask_ps(lt); "
  277:                         "uint32_t flags = ((lt_mask & 0x1) << 0) | ((gt_mask & 0x1) << 1) | "
  278:                         "((lt_mask & 0x2) << 1) | ((gt_mask & 0x2) << 2) | "
  279:                         "((lt_mask & 0x4) << 2) | ((gt_mask & 0x4) << 3); "
  280:                         "ctx->vu0_clip_flags = ((ctx->vu0_clip_flags << 6) | (flags & 0x3F)) & 0xFFFFFF; }}",
  281:                         inst.rd, inst.rt, inst.rt, shuffle_pattern);
  282:                 }
  283:                 case VU0_S2_VNOP:
  284:                     return fmt::format("// NOP operation, no action needed for VU0");
  285:                 case VU0_S2_VRNEXT:
  286:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VRNEXT(inst);
  287:                 case VU0_S2_VRGET:
  288:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VRGET(inst);
  289:                 case VU0_S2_VRINIT:
  290:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VRINIT(inst);
  291:                 case VU0_S2_VRXOR:
  292:                     return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VRXOR(inst);
  293:                 default:
  294:                     return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled VU0 Special2 function: 0x{:X}", vu_func));
  295:                 }
  296:             }
  297: 
  298:             // Special1 Table (function-based)
  299:             switch (special1_func)
  300:             {
  301:             case VU0_S1_VADDx:
  302:             case VU0_S1_VADDy:
  303:             case VU0_S1_VADDz:
  304:             case VU0_S1_VADDw:
  305:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADD_Field(inst);
  306:             case VU0_S1_VSUBx:
  307:             case VU0_S1_VSUBy:
  308:             case VU0_S1_VSUBz:
  309:             case VU0_S1_VSUBw:
  310:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUB_Field(inst);
  311:             case VU0_S1_VMULx:
  312:             case VU0_S1_VMULy:
  313:             case VU0_S1_VMULz:
  314:             case VU0_S1_VMULw:
  315:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMUL_Field(inst);
  316:             case VU0_S1_VADD:
  317:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADD(inst);
  318:             case VU0_S1_VSUB:
  319:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUB(inst);
  320:             case VU0_S1_VMUL:
  321:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMUL(inst);
  322:             case VU0_S1_VIADD:
  323:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VIADD(inst);
  324:             case VU0_S1_VISUB:
  325:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VISUB(inst);
  326:             case VU0_S1_VIADDI:
  327:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VIADDI(inst);
  328:             case VU0_S1_VIAND:
  329:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VIAND(inst);
  330:             case VU0_S1_VIOR:
  331:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VIOR(inst);
  332:             case VU0_S1_VCALLMS:
  333:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VCALLMS(inst);
  334:             case VU0_S1_VCALLMSR:
  335:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VCALLMSR(inst);
  336:             case VU0_S1_VADDq:
  337:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADDq(inst);
  338:             case VU0_S1_VSUBq:
  339:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUBq(inst);
  340:             case VU0_S1_VMULq:
  341:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMULq(inst);
  342:             case VU0_S1_VADDi:
  343:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VADDi(inst);
  344:             case VU0_S1_VSUBi:
  345:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VSUBi(inst);
  346:             case VU0_S1_VMULi:
  347:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMULi(inst);
  348:             case VU0_S1_VMADDx:
  349:             case VU0_S1_VMADDy:
  350:             case VU0_S1_VMADDz:
  351:             case VU0_S1_VMADDw:
  352:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADD_Field(inst);
  353:             case VU0_S1_VMSUBx:
  354:             case VU0_S1_VMSUBy:
  355:             case VU0_S1_VMSUBz:
  356:             case VU0_S1_VMSUBw:
  357:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUB_Field(inst);
  358:             case VU0_S1_VMAXx:
  359:             case VU0_S1_VMAXy:
  360:             case VU0_S1_VMAXz:
  361:             case VU0_S1_VMAXw:
  362:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMAX_Field(inst);
  363:             case VU0_S1_VMINIx:
  364:             case VU0_S1_VMINIy:
  365:             case VU0_S1_VMINIz:
  366:             case VU0_S1_VMINIw:
  367:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMINI_Field(inst);
  368:             case VU0_S1_VMAXi:
  369:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMAXi(inst);
  370:             case VU0_S1_VMINIi:
  371:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMINIi(inst);
  372:             case VU0_S1_VMADD:
  373:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADD(inst);
  374:             case VU0_S1_VMADDq:
  375:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADDq(inst);
  376:             case VU0_S1_VMADDi:
  377:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMADDi(inst);
  378:             case VU0_S1_VMAX:
  379:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMAX(inst);
  380:             case VU0_S1_VOPMSUB:
  381:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VOPMSUB(inst);
  382:             case VU0_S1_VMINI:
  383:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMINI(inst);
  384:             case VU0_S1_VMSUB:
  385:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUB(inst);
  386:             case VU0_S1_VMSUBq:
  387:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUBq(inst);
  388:             case VU0_S1_VMSUBi:
  389:                 return m_codeGenerator.translateVU_VMSUBi(inst);
  390:             default:
  391:                 return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled VU0 Special1 function: 0x{:X}", special1_func));
  392:             }
  393:         }
  394:         default:
  395:             return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled COP2 format: 0x{:X}", format));
  396:         }
  397:     }
  398: 
  399: }

LINES: 148
BYTES: 9166
    1: #include "ps2recomp/Translators/fpu_translator.h"
    2: #include "ps2recomp/code_generator.h"
    3: #include "ps2recomp/codegen_helpers.h"
    4: #include "ps2recomp/instructions.h"
    5: #include "ps2recomp/types.h"
    6: 
    7: #include <fmt/format.h>
    8: #include <sstream>
    9: #include <cmath>
   10: 
   11: namespace ps2recomp
   12: {
   13:     FpuTranslator::FpuTranslator(CodeGenerator &codeGenerator)
   14:         : m_codeGenerator(codeGenerator)
   15:     {
   16:     }
   17: 
   18:     std::string FpuTranslator::translate(const Instruction &inst)
   19:     {
   20:         uint8_t format = inst.rs; // Format field
   21:         uint32_t ft = inst.rt;    // FPU source register
   22:         uint32_t fs = inst.rd;    // FPU source register
   23:         uint32_t fd = inst.sa;    // FPU destination register
   24:         uint32_t function = inst.function;
   25: 
   26:         switch (format)
   27:         {
   28:         case COP1_MF:
   29:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t bits; std::memcpy(&bits, &ctx->f[{}], sizeof(bits)); SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, bits); }}", fs, ft);
   30:         case COP1_MT:
   31:             return fmt::format("{{ uint32_t bits = GPR_U32(ctx, {}); std::memcpy(&ctx->f[{}], &bits, sizeof(bits)); }}", ft, fs);
   32:         case COP1_CF:
   33:             if (fs == 31)
   34:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, ctx->fcr31);", ft); // FCR31 contains status/control
   35:             if (fs == 0)
   36:                 return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, 0x00000000);", ft); // FCR0 is the FPU implementation register
   37:             return fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, 0); // Unimplemented FCR{}", ft, fs);
   38:         case COP1_CT:
   39:             if (fs == 31)
   40:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = GPR_U32(ctx, {}) & 0x0183FFFF;", ft);
   41:             else
   42:                 return fmt::format("// CTC1 to FCR{} ignored", fs);
   43:             return "";
   44:         case COP1_BC:
   45:             return "// FPU branch instruction - handled elsewhere";
   46:         case COP1_S:
   47:             switch (function)
   48:             {
   49:             case COP1_S_ADD:
   50:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_ADD_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs, ft);
   51:             case COP1_S_SUB:
   52:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_SUB_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs, ft);
   53:             case COP1_S_MUL:
   54:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_MUL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs, ft);
   55:             case COP1_S_DIV:
   56:                 // The EE has no Inf/NaN: x/0 gives +-max (sign of fs xor ft), see ps2_fdiv.
   57:                 return fmt::format("{{ if (ctx->f[{}] == 0.0f) ctx->fcr31 |= 0x100000; /* DZ flag */ "
   58:                                    "ctx->f[{}] = FPU_DIV_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]); }}",
   59:                                    ft, fd, fs, ft);
   60:             case COP1_S_SQRT:
   61:                 // The R5900 encodes SQRT.S as fd, ft (fs is zero), unlike MIPS-I's fd, fs.
   62:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_SQRT_S(ctx->f[{}]);", fd, ft);
   63:             case COP1_S_ABS:
   64:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_ABS_S(ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs);
   65:             case COP1_S_MOV:
   66:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_MOV_S(ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs);
   67:             case COP1_S_NEG:
   68:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_NEG_S(ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs);
   69:             case COP1_S_ROUND_W:
   70:                 return fmt::format("{{ int32_t tmp = FPU_ROUND_W_S(ctx->f[{}]); std::memcpy(&ctx->f[{}], &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", fs, fd);
   71:             case COP1_S_TRUNC_W:
   72:                 return fmt::format("{{ int32_t tmp = FPU_TRUNC_W_S(ctx->f[{}]); std::memcpy(&ctx->f[{}], &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", fs, fd);
   73:             case COP1_S_CEIL_W:
   74:                 return fmt::format("{{ int32_t tmp = FPU_CEIL_W_S(ctx->f[{}]); std::memcpy(&ctx->f[{}], &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", fs, fd);
   75:             case COP1_S_FLOOR_W:
   76:                 return fmt::format("{{ int32_t tmp = FPU_FLOOR_W_S(ctx->f[{}]); std::memcpy(&ctx->f[{}], &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", fs, fd);
   77:             case COP1_S_CVT_W:
   78:                 return fmt::format("{{ int32_t tmp = FPU_CVT_W_S(ctx->f[{}]); std::memcpy(&ctx->f[{}], &tmp, sizeof(tmp)); }}", fs, fd);
   79:             case COP1_S_RSQRT:
   80:                 // RSQRT.S fd, fs, ft computes fs / sqrt(ft), not 1 / sqrt(fs).
   81:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_RSQRT_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs, ft);
   82:             case COP1_S_ADDA:
   83:                 return fmt::format("FPU_SET_ACC(ctx, FPU_ADD_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]));", fs, ft);
   84:             case COP1_S_SUBA:
   85:                 return fmt::format("FPU_SET_ACC(ctx, FPU_SUB_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]));", fs, ft);
   86:             case COP1_S_MULA:
   87:                 return fmt::format("FPU_SET_ACC(ctx, FPU_MUL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]));", fs, ft);
   88:             case COP1_S_MADD:
   89:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_ADD_S(ctx->f_acc, FPU_MUL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]));", fd, fs, ft);
   90:             case COP1_S_MSUB:
   91:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = FPU_SUB_S(ctx->f_acc, FPU_MUL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]));", fd, fs, ft);
   92:             case COP1_S_MADDA:
   93:                 return fmt::format("FPU_SET_ACC(ctx, FPU_ADD_S(ctx->f_acc, FPU_MUL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])));", fs, ft);
   94:             case COP1_S_MSUBA:
   95:                 return fmt::format("FPU_SET_ACC(ctx, FPU_SUB_S(ctx->f_acc, FPU_MUL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])));", fs, ft);
   96:             case COP1_S_MAX:
   97:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = std::max(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs, ft);
   98:             case COP1_S_MIN:
   99:                 return fmt::format("ctx->f[{}] = std::min(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}]);", fd, fs, ft);
  100:             case COP1_S_C_F:
  101:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 &= ~0x800000;");
  102:             case COP1_S_C_UN:
  103:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_UN_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  104:             case COP1_S_C_EQ:
  105:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_EQ_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  106:             case COP1_S_C_UEQ:
  107:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_UEQ_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  108:             case COP1_S_C_OLT:
  109:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_OLT_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  110:             case COP1_S_C_ULT:
  111:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_ULT_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  112:             case COP1_S_C_OLE:
  113:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_OLE_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  114:             case COP1_S_C_ULE:
  115:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_ULE_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  116:             case COP1_S_C_SF:
  117:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 &= ~0x800000;");
  118:             case COP1_S_C_NGLE:
  119:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_NGLE_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  120:             case COP1_S_C_SEQ:
  121:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_SEQ_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  122:             case COP1_S_C_NGL:
  123:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_NGL_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  124:             case COP1_S_C_LT:
  125:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_LT_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  126:             case COP1_S_C_NGE:
  127:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_NGE_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  128:             case COP1_S_C_LE:
  129:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_LE_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  130:             case COP1_S_C_NGT:
  131:                 return fmt::format("ctx->fcr31 = (FPU_C_NGT_S(ctx->f[{}], ctx->f[{}])) ? (ctx->fcr31 | 0x800000) : (ctx->fcr31 & ~0x800000);", fs, ft);
  132:             default:
  133:                 return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled FPU.S instruction: function 0x{:X}", function));
  134:             }
  135:         case COP1_W:
  136:             switch (function)
  137:             {
  138:             case COP1_W_CVT_S:
  139:                 return fmt::format("{{ int32_t tmp; std::memcpy(&tmp, &ctx->f[{}], sizeof(tmp)); ctx->f[{}] = FPU_CVT_S_W(tmp); }}", fs, fd);
  140:             default:
  141:                 return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled FPU.W instruction: function 0x{:X}", function));
  142:             }
  143:         default:
  144:             return m_codeGenerator.emitUnhandledInstruction(inst, fmt::format("Unhandled FPU instruction: format 0x{:X}, function 0x{:X}", format, function));
  145:         }
  146:     }
  147: 
  148: }

   14:         constexpr int kJumpTableBoundNearWindow = 30;
   15:         constexpr int kJumpTableBoundSearchLimit = 256;
   16: 
   17:         // Destination GPR of the integer instructions that can sit between a jump-table bound check and
   18:         // the table load; anything else is treated as not touching the index register.
   19:         bool writesGpr(const Instruction &inst, uint32_t reg)
   20:         {
   21:             if (reg == 0u || !inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR)
   22:             {
   23:                 return false;
   24:             }
   25:             switch (inst.opcode)
   26:             {
   27:             case OPCODE_SPECIAL:
   28:             case OPCODE_MMI:
   29:                 return inst.rd == reg;
   30:             case OPCODE_JAL:
   31:                 return reg == 31u;
   32:             default:
   33:                 return inst.rt == reg;
   34:             }
   35:         }
   36: 
   37:         // The delay slot of a branch-likely only runs when the branch is taken, so it cannot change a
   38:         // register on the fall-through path that reaches the jump-table load.
   39:         bool isBranchLikely(const Instruction &inst)
   40:         {
   41:             switch (inst.opcode)
   42:             {
   43:             case OPCODE_BEQL:
   44:             case OPCODE_BNEL:
   45:             case OPCODE_BLEZL:
   46:             case OPCODE_BGTZL:
   47:                 return true;
   48:             case OPCODE_REGIMM:
   49:                 return inst.rt == REGIMM_BLTZL || inst.rt == REGIMM_BGEZL ||
   50:                        inst.rt == REGIMM_BLTZALL || inst.rt == REGIMM_BGEZALL;
   51:             default:
   52:                 return false;
   53:             }
   54:         }
   55:     }
