   57:     ControlFlowAnalyzer::ControlFlowAnalyzer(
   58:         const std::vector<Section> &sections,
   59:         const std::unordered_map<uint32_t, std::vector<uint32_t>> &configuredJumpTableTargetsByAddress,
   60:         RecompilerReporter *reporter)
   61:         : m_sections(sections),
   62:           m_configJumpTableTargetsByAddress(configuredJumpTableTargetsByAddress),
   63:           m_reporter(reporter)
   64:     {
   65:     }
   66: 
   67:     ControlFlowAnalysisResult ControlFlowAnalyzer::analyze(
   68:         const Function &function,
   69:         const std::vector<Instruction> &instructions,
   70:         const std::vector<Function> *allFunctions) const
   71:     {
   72:         ControlFlowAnalysisResult result;
   73:         std::unordered_set<uint32_t> instructionAddresses;
   74:         instructionAddresses.reserve(instructions.size());
   75:         bool hasIndirectRegisterJump = false;
   76:         std::vector<const Instruction *> indirectJumps;
   77: 
   78:         auto isExecutableAddress = [&](uint32_t address) -> bool
   79:         {
   80:             for (const auto &section : m_sections)
   81:             {
   82:                 if (!section.isCode)
   83:                 {
   84:                     continue;
   85:                 }
   86:                 if (address >= section.address && address < (section.address + section.size))
   87:                 {
   88:                     return true;
   89:                 }
   90:             }
   91:             return false;
   92:         };
   93: 
   94:         auto findContainingExternalFunction = [&](uint32_t address) -> const Function *
   95:         {
   96:             if (!allFunctions || !isExecutableAddress(address))
   97:             {
   98:                 return nullptr;
   99:             }
  100: 
  101:             const Function *best = nullptr;
  102:             for (const auto &candidateFn : *allFunctions)
  103:             {
  104:                 if (!candidateFn.isRecompiled || candidateFn.isStub || candidateFn.isSkipped)
  105:                 {
  106:                     continue;
  107:                 }
  108: 
  109:                 if (candidateFn.name.rfind("entry_", 0) == 0)
  110:                 {
  111:                     continue;
  112:                 }
  113: 
  114:                 if (address < candidateFn.start || address >= candidateFn.end)
  115:                 {
  116:                     continue;
  117:                 }
  118: 
  119:                 if (!best || candidateFn.start > best->start)
  120:                 {
  121:                     best = &candidateFn;
  122:                 }
  123:             }
  124: 
  125:             return best;
  126:         };
  127: 
  128:         auto queueExternalEntryTarget = [&](uint32_t target)
  129:         {
  130:             const Function *containingFn = findContainingExternalFunction(target);
  131:             if (!containingFn)
  132:             {
  133:                 return;
  134:             }
  135: 
  136:             if (containingFn->start == function.start)
  137:             {
  138:                 return;
  139:             }
  140: 
  141:             if (target == containingFn->start)
  142:             {
  143:                 return;
  144:             }
  145: 
  146:             result.externalEntryPoints.insert(target);
  147:         };
  148: 
  149:         auto queueResumeEntryTarget = [&](uint32_t resumeAddr)
  150:         {
  151:             if (resumeAddr >= function.start && resumeAddr < function.end &&
  152:                 instructionAddresses.contains(resumeAddr))
  153:             {
  154:                 result.entryPoints.insert(resumeAddr);
  155:                 result.resumeEntryPoints.insert(resumeAddr);
  156:             }
  157:         };
  158: 
  159:         auto queueLoopResumeEntryTarget = [&](uint32_t target, uint32_t sourcePc)
  160:         {
  161:             if (target > sourcePc || target == function.start)
  162:             {
  163:                 return;
  164:             }
  165: 
  166:             queueResumeEntryTarget(target);
  167:         };
  168: 
  169:         for (const auto &inst : instructions)
  170:         {
  171:             instructionAddresses.insert(inst.address);
  172:             if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SPECIAL &&
  173:                 ((inst.function == SPECIAL_JR && inst.rs != 31) ||
  174:                  inst.function == SPECIAL_JALR))
  175:             {
  176:                 hasIndirectRegisterJump = true;
  177:                 indirectJumps.push_back(&inst);
  178:             }
  179:         }
  180: 
  181:         for (const auto &inst : instructions)
  182:         {
  183:             // A guest-installed syscall handler runs as a separate invocation,
  184:             // so the scheduler resumes this thread at syscall+4 and needs an
  185:             // entry point there. +4, not +8: syscall has no delay slot.
  186:             if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SPECIAL && inst.function == SPECIAL_SYSCALL)
  187:             {
  188:                 queueResumeEntryTarget(inst.address + 4u);
  189:             }
  190: 
  191:             bool isStaticJump = (inst.opcode == OPCODE_J || inst.opcode == OPCODE_JAL);
  192:             if (inst.isBranch && inst.opcode != OPCODE_J && inst.opcode != OPCODE_JAL)
  193:             {
  194:                 const int32_t offsetBytes = (static_cast<int32_t>(static_cast<int16_t>(inst.simmediate)) << 2);
  195:                 const uint32_t target = static_cast<uint32_t>(
  196:                     static_cast<int64_t>(inst.address + 4u) + static_cast<int64_t>(offsetBytes));
  197: 
  198:                 if (target >= function.start && target < function.end &&
  199:                     instructionAddresses.contains(target))
  200:                 {
  201:                     result.entryPoints.insert(target);
  202:                     queueLoopResumeEntryTarget(target, inst.address);
  203:                 }
  204:                 else
  205:                 {
  206:                     queueExternalEntryTarget(target);
  207:                 }
  208:             }
  209:             else if (isStaticJump)
  210:             {
  211:                 uint32_t target = buildAbsoluteJumpTarget(inst.address, inst.target);
  212:                 if (target >= function.start && target < function.end &&
  213:                     instructionAddresses.contains(target))
  214:                 {
  215:                     result.entryPoints.insert(target);
  216:                     queueLoopResumeEntryTarget(target, inst.address);
  217: 
  218:                     if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_JAL)
  219:                     {
  220:                         queueResumeEntryTarget(inst.address + 8u);
  221:                     }
  222:                 }
  223:                 else
  224:                 {
  225:                     queueExternalEntryTarget(target);
  226: 
  227:                     if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_JAL)
  228:                     {
  229:                         queueResumeEntryTarget(inst.address + 8u);
  230:                     }
  231:                 }
  232:             }
  233:         }
  234: 
  235:         if (hasIndirectRegisterJump)
  236:         {
  237:             bool needsIndirectFallback = false;
  238:             for (const Instruction *jrInst : indirectJumps)
  239:             {
  240:                 if (jrInst->function == SPECIAL_JALR)
  241:                 {
  242:                     queueResumeEntryTarget(jrInst->address + 8u);
  243:                 }
  244: 
  245:                 bool foundTable = false;
  246: 
  247:                 uint32_t jrReg = jrInst->rs;
  248: 
  249:                 int lwIndex = -1;
  250:                 uint32_t baseReg = 0;
  251:                 int32_t lwOffset = 0;
  252: 
  253:                 auto it = std::find_if(instructions.begin(), instructions.end(), [&](const Instruction &inst)
  254:                                        { return inst.address == jrInst->address; });
  255:                 if (it != instructions.end())
  256:                 {
  257:                     int jrIndex = std::distance(instructions.begin(), it);
  258:                     for (int i = jrIndex - 1; i >= 0 && i >= jrIndex - 20; --i)
  259:                     {
  260:                         const auto &inst = instructions[i];
  261:                         if ((inst.opcode == OPCODE_LW || inst.opcode == OPCODE_LWU) && inst.rt == jrReg)
  262:                         {
  263:                             lwIndex = i;
  264:                             baseReg = inst.rs;
  265:                             lwOffset = inst.simmediate;
  266:                             break;
  267:                         }
  268:                     }
  269: 
  270:                     if (lwIndex != -1)
  271:                     {
  272:                         int adduIndex = -1;
  273:                         uint32_t tableBaseReg = 0;
  274:                         uint32_t indexReg = 0;
  275:                         for (int i = lwIndex - 1; i >= 0 && i >= lwIndex - 10; --i)
  276:                         {
  277:                             const auto &inst = instructions[i];
  278:                             if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SPECIAL && inst.function == SPECIAL_ADDU && inst.rd == baseReg)
  279:                             {
  280:                                 adduIndex = i;
  281:                                 tableBaseReg = inst.rs;
  282:                                 indexReg = inst.rt;
  283:                                 break;
  284:                             }
  285:                         }
  286: 
  287:                         uint32_t tableAddress = 0;
  288:                         bool foundTableAddress = false;
  289: 
  290:                         if (adduIndex != -1)
  291:                         {
  292:                             for (int i = adduIndex - 1; i >= 0 && i >= adduIndex - 20; --i)
  293:                             {
  294:                                 const auto &inst = instructions[i];
  295:                                 if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_LUI)
  296:                                 {
  297:                                     if (inst.rt == tableBaseReg || inst.rt == indexReg)
  298:                                     {
  299:                                         uint32_t high = inst.immediate << 16;
  300:                                         uint32_t low = 0;
  301:                                         for (int j = i + 1; j < adduIndex; ++j)
  302:                                         {
  303:                                             const auto &lowInst = instructions[j];
  304:                                             if (lowInst.rs == inst.rt && lowInst.rt == inst.rt)
  305:                                             {
  306:                                                 if (lowInst.opcode == OPCODE_ADDIU)
  307:                                                 {
  308:                                                     low = (uint32_t)lowInst.simmediate;
  309:                                                 }
  310:                                                 else if (lowInst.opcode == OPCODE_ORI)
  311:                                                 {
  312:                                                     low = lowInst.immediate;
  313:                                                 }
  314:                                             }
  315:                                         }
  316:                                         tableAddress = high + low;
  317:                                         foundTableAddress = true;
  318:                                         break;
  319:                                     }
  320:                                 }
  321:                             }
  322:                         }
  323: 
  324:                         if (foundTableAddress)
  325:                         {
  326:                             tableAddress += lwOffset;
  327: 
  328:                             const auto configuredTableIt = m_configJumpTableTargetsByAddress.find(tableAddress);
  329:                             if (configuredTableIt != m_configJumpTableTargetsByAddress.end())
  330:                             {
  331:                                 std::vector<uint32_t> jrTargets;
  332:                                 jrTargets.reserve(configuredTableIt->second.size());
  333:                                 for (uint32_t target : configuredTableIt->second)
  334:                                 {
  335:                                     if (target >= function.start && target < function.end &&
  336:                                         instructionAddresses.contains(target))
  337:                                     {
  338:                                         jrTargets.push_back(target);
  339:                                     }
  340:                                     else
  341:                                     {
  342:                                         queueExternalEntryTarget(target);
  343:                                     }
  344:                                 }
  345: 
  346:                                 if (!jrTargets.empty())
  347:                                 {
  348:                                     std::sort(jrTargets.begin(), jrTargets.end());
  349:                                     jrTargets.erase(std::unique(jrTargets.begin(), jrTargets.end()), jrTargets.end());
  350:                                     result.jumpTableTargets[jrInst->address] = jrTargets;
  351:                                     for (uint32_t target : jrTargets)
  352:                                     {
  353:                                         result.entryPoints.insert(target);
  354:                                     }
  355:                                     foundTable = true;
  356:                                 }
  357:                             }
  358: 
  359:                             uint32_t unshiftedIndexReg = 0;
  360:                             int sllIndex = -1;
  361:                             for (int i = adduIndex - 1; i >= 0 && i >= adduIndex - 10; --i)
  362:                             {
  363:                                 const auto &inst = instructions[i];
  364:                                 if (inst.opcode == OPCODE_SPECIAL && inst.function == SPECIAL_SLL && (inst.rd == tableBaseReg || inst.rd == indexReg))
  365:                                 {
  366:                                     unshiftedIndexReg = inst.rt;
  367:                                     sllIndex = i;
  368:                                     break;
  369:                                 }
  370:                             }
  371: 
  372:                             uint32_t numCases = 0;
  373:                             if (unshiftedIndexReg != 0)
  374:                             {
  375:                                 for (int i = adduIndex - 1; i >= 0 && i >= adduIndex - kJumpTableBoundNearWindow; --i)
  376:                                 {
  377:                                     const auto &inst = instructions[i];
  378:                                     if ((inst.opcode == OPCODE_SLTIU || inst.opcode == OPCODE_SLTI) && inst.rs == unshiftedIndexReg)
  379:                                     {
  380:                                         numCases = inst.immediate;
  381:                                         break;
  382:                                     }
  383:                                 }
  384:                             }
  385:                             if (numCases == 0 && sllIndex > 0)
  386:                             {
  387:                                 // GCC can hoist the bound check far above the table load when it schedules a
  388:                                 // long block in between. Past the near window, keep walking back only until
  389:                                 // an instruction writes the index register, so a compare on an older value is
  390:                                 // never taken as the bound. Start at the SLL: the index register is often
  391:                                 // reused for the table base after it.
  392:                                 for (int i = sllIndex - 1; i >= 0 && i >= sllIndex - kJumpTableBoundSearchLimit; --i)
  393:                                 {
  394:                                     const auto &inst = instructions[i];
  395:                                     if ((inst.opcode == OPCODE_SLTIU || inst.opcode == OPCODE_SLTI) && inst.rs == unshiftedIndexReg)
  396:                                     {
  397:                                         numCases = inst.immediate;
  398:                                         break;
  399:                                     }
  400:                                     if (writesGpr(inst, unshiftedIndexReg) &&
  401:                                         !(i > 0 && isBranchLikely(instructions[i - 1])))
  402:                                     {
  403:                                         break;
  404:                                     }
  405:                                 }
  406:                             }
  407: 
  408:                             if (!foundTable && numCases > 0 && numCases <= 1000)
  409:                             {
  410:                                 const Section *rodata = nullptr;
  411:                                 for (const auto &sec : m_sections)
  412:                                 {
  413:                                     if (tableAddress >= sec.address && tableAddress < sec.address + sec.size)
  414:                                     {
  415:                                         rodata = &sec;
  416:                                         break;
  417:                                     }
  418:                                 }
  419: 
  420:                                 if (rodata && rodata->data)
  421:                                 {
  422:                                     std::vector<uint32_t> jrTargets;
  423:                                     bool validJumpTable = true;
  424:                                     std::unordered_set<uint32_t> uniqueTargets;
  425:                                     for (uint32_t i = 0; i < numCases; ++i)
  426:                                     {
  427:                                         uint32_t addr = tableAddress + i * 4;
  428:                                         if (addr >= rodata->address && addr + 4 <= rodata->address + rodata->size)
  429:                                         {
  430:                                             uint32_t target = 0;
  431:                                             std::memcpy(&target, rodata->data + (addr - rodata->address), 4);
  432:                                             if (target >= function.start && target < function.end && instructionAddresses.contains(target))
  433:                                             {
  434:                                                 if (!uniqueTargets.contains(target))
  435:                                                 {
  436:                                                     jrTargets.push_back(target);
  437:                                                     uniqueTargets.insert(target);
  438:                                                 }
  439:                                             }
  440:                                             else
  441:                                             {
  442:                                                 queueExternalEntryTarget(target);
  443:                                             }
  444:                                         }
  445:                                         else
  446:                                         {
  447:                                             validJumpTable = false;
  448:                                             break;
  449:                                         }
  450:                                     }
  451:                                     if (validJumpTable && !jrTargets.empty())
  452:                                     {
  453:                                         result.jumpTableTargets[jrInst->address] = jrTargets;
  454:                                         for (uint32_t t : jrTargets)
  455:                                         {
  456:                                             result.entryPoints.insert(t);
  457:                                         }
  458:                                         foundTable = true;
  459:                                     }
  460:                                 }
  461:                             }
  462:                         }
  463:                     }
  464:                 }
  465:                 if (!foundTable)
  466:                 {
  467:                     needsIndirectFallback = true;
  468:                 }
  469:             }
  470: 
  471:             if (needsIndirectFallback)
  472:             {
  473:                 if (m_reporter)
  474:                 {
  475:                     std::vector<uint32_t> jumpAddresses;
  476:                     jumpAddresses.reserve(indirectJumps.size());
  477:                     for (const Instruction *jrInst : indirectJumps)
  478:                     {
  479:                         jumpAddresses.push_back(jrInst->address);
  480:                     }
  481:                     m_reporter->recordIndirectFallbackPromotion(function.name, jumpAddresses, instructionAddresses.size());
  482:                 }
  483: 
  484:                 for (uint32_t addr : instructionAddresses)
  485:                 {
  486:                     if (addr >= function.start && addr < function.end)
  487:                     {
  488:                         result.entryPoints.insert(addr);
  489:                         // Keep labels and runtime registration for unresolved JR/JALR targets
  490:                         // without emitting a local switch over every possible target.
  491:                         result.indirectFallbackEntryPoints.insert(addr);
  492:                     }
  493:                 }
  494:             }
  495:         }
  496: 
  497:         return result;
  498:     }
  499: 
  500: }

   61:     bool ControlFlowEmitter::isLikelyBranch() const
   62:     {
   63:         return (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_BEQL || m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_BNEL ||
   64:                 m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_BLEZL || m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_BGTZL ||
   65:                 (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_REGIMM &&
   66:                  (m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BLTZL || m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BGEZL ||
   67:                   m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BLTZALL || m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BGEZALL)) ||
   68:                 (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_COP1 && m_branchInst.rs == COP1_BC &&
   69:                  (m_branchInst.rt == COP1_BC_BCFL || m_branchInst.rt == COP1_BC_BCTL)) ||
   70:                 (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_COP2 && m_branchInst.rs == COP2_BC &&
   71:                  (m_branchInst.rt == COP2_BC_BCFL || m_branchInst.rt == COP2_BC_BCTL)));
   72:     }

   74:     std::vector<uint32_t> ControlFlowEmitter::resolvedLocalIndirectTargets() const
   75:     {
   76:         if (m_branchInst.opcode != OPCODE_SPECIAL)
   77:         {
   78:             return {};
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         if (!((m_branchInst.function == SPECIAL_JR && m_branchInst.rs != 31u) ||
   82:               m_branchInst.function == SPECIAL_JALR))
   83:         {
   84:             return {};
   85:         }
   86: 
   87:         auto jtIt = m_analysisResult.jumpTableTargets.find(m_branchInst.address);
   88:         if (jtIt == m_analysisResult.jumpTableTargets.end())
   89:         {
   90:             return {};
   91:         }
   92: 
   93:         std::vector<uint32_t> targets = jtIt->second;
   94:         std::sort(targets.begin(), targets.end());
   95:         targets.erase(std::unique(targets.begin(), targets.end()), targets.end());
   96:         return targets;
   97:     }

   99:     std::string ControlFlowEmitter::delaySlotCode() const
  100:     {
  101:         if (!hasRealDelaySlot())
  102:         {
  103:             return {};
  104:         }
  105: 
  106:         if (!m_delaySlotOverride.empty())
  107:         {
  108:             return m_delaySlotOverride;
  109:         }
  110: 
  111:         std::string code;
  112:         if (m_gen.m_emitInstructionComments)
  113:         {
  114:             code = "// 0x" + fmt::format("{:x}", m_delaySlot.address) + ": 0x" + fmt::format("{:x}", m_delaySlot.raw);
  115:             std::string disassembly = R5900Decoder::disassembleInstruction(m_delaySlot);
  116:             if (!disassembly.empty())
  117:             {
  118:                 code += "  " + disassembly;
  119:             }
  120:             code += " (Delay Slot)\n";
  121:         }
  122: 
  123:         code += m_gen.translateInstruction(m_delaySlot);
  124:         return code;
  125:     }

  127:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitDelaySlot(std::string_view indent)
  128:     {
  129:         if (!hasRealDelaySlot())
  130:         {
  131:             return;
  132:         }
  133: 
  134:         m_ss << fmt::format("{}ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", indent, delayPc());
  135:         m_ss << fmt::format("{}ctx->in_delay_slot = true;\n", indent);
  136:         m_ss << fmt::format("{}ctx->branch_pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", indent, branchPc());
  137: 
  138:         const std::string code = delaySlotCode();
  139:         std::istringstream lines(code);
  140:         std::string line;
  141:         while (std::getline(lines, line))
  142:         {
  143:             if (!line.empty())
  144:             {
  145:                 m_ss << indent << line << "\n";
  146:             }
  147:         }
  148: 
  149:         m_ss << fmt::format("{}ctx->in_delay_slot = false;\n", indent);
  150:     }
  151: 
  152:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitResumeFromDelaySlotEntry()
  153:     {
  154:         if (!isInternalTarget(delayPc()))
  155:         {
  156:             return;
  157:         }
  158: 
  159:         m_ss << fmt::format("    if (ctx->pc == 0x{:X}u) {{\n", delayPc());
  160:         emitDelaySlot("        ");
  161:         m_ss << fmt::format("        ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", fallthroughPc());
  162: 
  163:         if (isInternalTarget(fallthroughPc()))
  164:         {
  165:             m_ss << fmt::format("        goto label_{:x};\n", fallthroughPc());
  166:         }
  167:         else
  168:         {
  169:             m_ss << fmt::format("        goto label_fallthrough_0x{:x};\n", branchPc());
  170:         }
  171: 
  172:         m_ss << "    }\n";
  173:     }

  175:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitInternalTarget(uint32_t target, uint32_t sourcePc, std::string_view indent)
  176:     {
  177:         m_ss << fmt::format("{}ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", indent, target);
  178:         if (target <= sourcePc && !isCallLikeEdge())
  179:         {
  180:             m_ss << fmt::format("{}if (runtime->eeCheckpointDue()) {{\n", indent);
  181:             m_ss << fmt::format("{}    return;\n", indent);
  182:             m_ss << fmt::format("{}}}\n", indent);
  183:         }
  184:         m_ss << fmt::format("{}goto label_{:x};\n", indent, target);
  185:     }

  187:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitRuntimeBranchDispatch(std::string_view targetExpression,
  188:                                                        uint32_t sourcePc,

  191:                                                        std::string_view debugName,

  210:     bool ControlFlowEmitter::emitDirectFunctionJumpIfAvailable(uint32_t target, StaticBranchKind kind, std::string_view indent)
  211:     {
  212:         if (kind != StaticBranchKind::Jump)
  213:         {
  214:             return false;
  215:         }
  216: 
  217:         const std::string functionName = m_gen.getFunctionName(target);
  218:         if (functionName.empty())
  219:         {
  220:             return false;
  221:         }
  222: 
  223:         m_ss << indent << functionName << "(rdram, ctx, runtime); return;\n";
  224:         return true;
  225:     }

  227:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitExternalJumpDispatch(uint32_t target, StaticBranchKind kind, std::string_view indent)
  228:     {
  229:         const bool isCall = kind == StaticBranchKind::Call;
  230:         emitRuntimeBranchDispatch(fmt::format("0x{:X}u", target),
  231:                                   branchPc(),
  232:                                   isCall ? fallthroughPc() : 0u,
  233:                                   isCall ? "DirectCall" : "DirectJump",
  234:                                   isCall ? "JAL" : "J",
  235:                                   indent,
  236:                                   true);
  237:     }

  239:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitExternalRegisterCallDispatch(std::string_view jumpTargetExpression, std::string_view indent)
  240:     {
  241:         emitRuntimeBranchDispatch(jumpTargetExpression,
  242:                                   branchPc(),
  243:                                   fallthroughPc(),
  244:                                   "IndirectCall",
  245:                                   "JALR",
  246:                                   indent,
  247:                                   true);
  248:     }
  249: 
  250:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitExternalRegisterJumpDispatch(std::string_view jumpTargetExpression,
  251:                                                               RegisterBranchKind kind,
  252:                                                               uint8_t rsReg,
  253:                                                               std::string_view indent)
  254:     {
  255:         const bool isReturn = kind == RegisterBranchKind::Jump && rsReg == 31u;
  256: 
  257:         if (isReturn)
  258:         {
  259:             m_ss << indent << "#if defined(PS2X_STRICT_RETURN_DIAGNOSTICS) && PS2X_STRICT_RETURN_DIAGNOSTICS\n";
  260:             m_ss << indent << "(void)runtime->dispatchGuestBranch(rdram, ctx, " << jumpTargetExpression
  261:                  << ", 0x" << fmt::format("{:X}", branchPc())
  262:                  << "u, 0u, PS2Runtime::GuestBranchKind::Return, \"JR $ra\");\n";
  263:             m_ss << indent << "return;\n";
  264:             m_ss << indent << "#else\n";
  265:             m_ss << indent << "ctx->pc = " << jumpTargetExpression << ";\n";
  266:             m_ss << indent << "return;\n";
  267:             m_ss << indent << "#endif\n";
  268:             return;
  269:         }
  270: 
  271:         emitRuntimeBranchDispatch(jumpTargetExpression,
  272:                                   branchPc(),
  273:                                   0u,
  274:                                   "IndirectJump",
  275:                                   "JR",
  276:                                   indent,
  277:                                   true);
  278:     }
  279: 
  280:     bool ControlFlowEmitter::emitRelocationCallIfAvailable(StaticBranchKind kind, std::string_view indent)
  281:     {
  282:         const auto relocIt = m_gen.m_relocationCallNames.find(m_branchInst.address);
  283:         if (relocIt == m_gen.m_relocationCallNames.end() || relocIt->second.empty())
  284:         {
  285:             return false;
  286:         }
  287: 
  288:         const std::string_view resolvedSyscallName = ps2_runtime_calls::resolveSyscallName(relocIt->second);
  289:         const std::string_view resolvedStubName = ps2_runtime_calls::resolveStubName(relocIt->second);
  290:         if (resolvedSyscallName.empty() && resolvedStubName.empty())
  291:         {
  292:             return false;
  293:         }
  294: 
  295:         const bool isSyscall = !resolvedSyscallName.empty();
  296:         const std::string_view handlerName = isSyscall ? resolvedSyscallName : resolvedStubName;
  297: 
  298:         m_ss << indent << "{\n";
  299:         if (kind == StaticBranchKind::Call)
  300:         {
  301:             m_ss << fmt::format("{}    ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", indent, fallthroughPc());
  302:         }
  303:         else
  304:         {
  305:             m_ss << indent << "    ctx->pc = getRegU32(ctx, 31);\n";
  306:         }
  307:         m_ss << indent << "    " << (isSyscall ? "ps2_syscalls::" : "ps2_stubs::")
  308:              << handlerName << "(rdram, ctx, runtime);\n";
  309:         m_ss << indent << "}\n";
  310: 
  311:         if (kind == StaticBranchKind::Jump)
  312:         {
  313:             m_ss << indent << "return;\n";
  314:         }
  315:         else
  316:         {
  317:             m_ss << fmt::format("{}if (ctx->pc != 0x{:X}u) {{ return; }}\n", indent, fallthroughPc());
  318:         }
  319: 
  320:         return true;
  321:     }
  322: 
  323:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitStaticJump(StaticBranchKind kind)
  324:     {
  325:         if (kind == StaticBranchKind::Call)
  326:         {
  327:             m_ss << fmt::format("    SET_GPR_U32(ctx, 31, 0x{:X}u);\n", fallthroughPc());
  328:         }
  329: 
  330:         emitDelaySlot("    ");
  331: 
  332:         const uint32_t target = buildAbsoluteJumpTarget(m_branchInst.address, m_branchInst.target);
  333:         if (isInternalTarget(target))
  334:         {
  335:             emitInternalTarget(target, branchPc(), "    ");
  336:             return;
  337:         }
  338: 
  339:         m_ss << fmt::format("    ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", target);
  340: 
  341:         if (kind == StaticBranchKind::Call && emitRelocationCallIfAvailable(kind, "    "))
  342:         {
  343:             return;
  344:         }
  345: 
  346:         if (emitDirectFunctionJumpIfAvailable(target, kind, "    "))
  347:         {
  348:             return;
  349:         }
  350: 
  351:         emitExternalJumpDispatch(target, kind, "    ");
  352:     }
  353: 
  354:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitRegisterJump(RegisterBranchKind kind)
  355:     {
  356:         const uint8_t rsReg = static_cast<uint8_t>(m_branchInst.rs);
  357:         const uint8_t rdReg = static_cast<uint8_t>(m_branchInst.rd);
  358:         const std::vector<uint32_t> sortedInternalTargets = resolvedLocalIndirectTargets();
  359: 
  360:         m_ss << "    {\n";
  361:         m_ss << "        const uint32_t jumpTarget = GPR_U32(ctx, " << static_cast<int>(rsReg) << ");\n";
  362: 
  363:         if (kind == RegisterBranchKind::Call && rdReg != 0u)
  364:         {
  365:             m_ss << fmt::format("        SET_GPR_U32(ctx, {}, 0x{:X}u);\n", rdReg, fallthroughPc());
  366:         }
  367: 
  368:         emitDelaySlot("        ");
  369:         m_ss << "        ctx->pc = jumpTarget;\n";
  370: 
  371:         if (!sortedInternalTargets.empty())
  372:         {
  373:             m_ss << "        switch (jumpTarget) {\n";
  374:             for (uint32_t target : sortedInternalTargets)
  375:             {
  376:                 m_ss << fmt::format("            case 0x{:X}u: goto label_{:x};\n", target, target);
  377:             }
  378:             m_ss << "            default: break;\n";
  379:             m_ss << "        }\n";
  380:         }
  381: 
  382:         if (kind == RegisterBranchKind::Jump)
  383:         {
  384:             emitExternalRegisterJumpDispatch("jumpTarget", kind, rsReg, "        ");
  385:         }
  386:         else
  387:         {
  388:             emitExternalRegisterCallDispatch("jumpTarget", "        ");
  389:         }
  390: 
  391:         m_ss << "    }\n";
  392:     }
  393: 
  394:     std::string ControlFlowEmitter::conditionalBranchExpression() const
  395:     {
  396:         const uint8_t rsReg = static_cast<uint8_t>(m_branchInst.rs);
  397:         const uint8_t rtReg = static_cast<uint8_t>(m_branchInst.rt);
  398: 
  399:         switch (m_branchInst.opcode)
  400:         {
  401:         case OPCODE_BEQ:
  402:         case OPCODE_BEQL:
  403:             return fmt::format("GPR_U64(ctx, {}) == GPR_U64(ctx, {})", rsReg, rtReg);
  404:         case OPCODE_BNE:
  405:         case OPCODE_BNEL:
  406:             return fmt::format("GPR_U64(ctx, {}) != GPR_U64(ctx, {})", rsReg, rtReg);
  407:         // Sign branches test all 64 bits: an ld of an IPU register yields 0x00000000_8xxxxxxx, which is positive.
  408:         case OPCODE_BLEZ:
  409:         case OPCODE_BLEZL:
  410:             return fmt::format("GPR_S64(ctx, {}) <= 0", rsReg);
  411:         case OPCODE_BGTZ:
  412:         case OPCODE_BGTZL:
  413:             return fmt::format("GPR_S64(ctx, {}) > 0", rsReg);
  414:         case OPCODE_REGIMM:
  415:             switch (m_branchInst.rt)
  416:             {
  417:             case REGIMM_BLTZ:
  418:             case REGIMM_BLTZL:
  419:             case REGIMM_BLTZAL:
  420:             case REGIMM_BLTZALL:
  421:                 return fmt::format("GPR_S64(ctx, {}) < 0", rsReg);
  422:             case REGIMM_BGEZ:
  423:             case REGIMM_BGEZL:
  424:             case REGIMM_BGEZAL:
  425:             case REGIMM_BGEZALL:
  426:                 return fmt::format("GPR_S64(ctx, {}) >= 0", rsReg);
  427:             default:
  428:                 return "false";
  429:             }
  430:         case OPCODE_COP1:
  431:             if (m_branchInst.rs == COP1_BC)
  432:             {
  433:                 const uint8_t bcCond = static_cast<uint8_t>(m_branchInst.rt);
  434:                 return (bcCond == COP1_BC_BCF || bcCond == COP1_BC_BCFL)
  435:                            ? "!(ctx->fcr31 & 0x800000)"
  436:                            : "(ctx->fcr31 & 0x800000)";
  437:             }
  438:             break;
  439:         case OPCODE_COP2:
  440:             if (m_branchInst.rs == COP2_BC)
  441:             {
  442:                 const uint8_t bcCond = static_cast<uint8_t>(m_branchInst.rt);
  443:                 return (bcCond == COP2_BC_BCF || bcCond == COP2_BC_BCFL)
  444:                            ? "!(ctx->vu0_status & 0x1)"
  445:                            : "(ctx->vu0_status & 0x1)";
  446:             }
  447:             break;
  448:         default:
  449:             break;
  450:         }
  451: 
  452:         return "false";
  453:     }
  454: 
  455:     uint32_t ControlFlowEmitter::conditionalBranchTarget() const
  456:     {
  457:         const int32_t offsetBytes = static_cast<int32_t>(static_cast<int16_t>(m_branchInst.simmediate)) << 2;
  458:         return static_cast<uint32_t>(static_cast<int64_t>(m_branchInst.address + 4u) +
  459:                                      static_cast<int64_t>(offsetBytes));
  460:     }
  461: 
  462:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitConditionalBranch()
  463:     {
  464:         const uint32_t target = conditionalBranchTarget();
  465:         const bool likely = isLikelyBranch();
  466:         const std::string branchTakenVar = fmt::format("branch_taken_0x{:x}", m_branchInst.address);
  467:         std::string unconditionalLinkCode;
  468:         std::string conditionalLinkCode;
  469: 
  470:         if (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_REGIMM)
  471:         {
  472:             if (m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BLTZAL || m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BGEZAL)
  473:             {
  474:                 unconditionalLinkCode = fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, 31, 0x{:X}u);", fallthroughPc());
  475:             }
  476:             else if (m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BLTZALL || m_branchInst.rt == REGIMM_BGEZALL)
  477:             {
  478:                 conditionalLinkCode = fmt::format("SET_GPR_U32(ctx, 31, 0x{:X}u);", fallthroughPc());
  479:             }
  480:         }
  481: 
  482:         m_ss << "    {\n";
  483:         m_ss << "        const bool " << branchTakenVar << " = (" << conditionalBranchExpression() << ");\n";
  484: 
  485:         if (!unconditionalLinkCode.empty())
  486:         {
  487:             m_ss << "        " << unconditionalLinkCode << "\n";
  488:         }
  489: 
  490:         if (likely)
  491:         {
  492:             m_ss << "        if (" << branchTakenVar << ") {\n";
  493:             if (!conditionalLinkCode.empty())
  494:             {
  495:                 m_ss << "            " << conditionalLinkCode << "\n";
  496:             }
  497:             emitDelaySlot("            ");
  498: 
  499:             if (isInternalTarget(target))
  500:             {
  501:                 emitInternalTarget(target, branchPc(), "            ");
  502:             }
  503:             else
  504:             {
  505:                 m_ss << fmt::format("            ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", target);
  506:                 m_ss << "            return;\n";
  507:             }
  508:             m_ss << "        }\n";
  509:         }
  510:         else
  511:         {
  512:             if (!conditionalLinkCode.empty())
  513:             {
  514:                 m_ss << "        if (" << branchTakenVar << ") { " << conditionalLinkCode << " }\n";
  515:             }
  516: 
  517:             emitDelaySlot("        ");
  518: 
  519:             m_ss << "        if (" << branchTakenVar << ") {\n";
  520:             if (isInternalTarget(target))
  521:             {
  522:                 emitInternalTarget(target, branchPc(), "            ");
  523:             }
  524:             else
  525:             {
  526:                 m_ss << fmt::format("            ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", target);
  527:                 m_ss << "            return;\n";
  528:             }
  529:             m_ss << "        }\n";
  530:         }
  531: 
  532:         m_ss << "    }\n";
  533:     }
  534: 
  535:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitFallbackInstruction()
  536:     {
  537:         m_ss << "    " << m_gen.translateInstruction(m_branchInst) << "\n";
  538:         emitDelaySlot("    ");
  539:     }
  540: 
  541:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitFallthroughLabelIfNeeded()
  542:     {
  543:         if (isInternalTarget(delayPc()) && !isInternalTarget(fallthroughPc()))
  544:         {
  545:             m_ss << fmt::format("label_fallthrough_0x{:x}:\n", branchPc());
  546:         }
  547:     }
  548: 
  549:     void ControlFlowEmitter::emitFinalFallthrough()
  550:     {
  551:         m_ss << fmt::format("    ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", fallthroughPc());
  552:     }
  553: 
  554:     std::string ControlFlowEmitter::emit()
  555:     {
  556:         (void)m_function;
  557:         emitResumeFromDelaySlotEntry();
  558:         m_ss << fmt::format("    ctx->pc = 0x{:X}u;\n", branchPc());
  559: 
  560:         if (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_J || m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_JAL)
  561:         {
  562:             emitStaticJump(m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_JAL ? StaticBranchKind::Call : StaticBranchKind::Jump);
  563:         }
  564:         else if (m_branchInst.opcode == OPCODE_SPECIAL &&
  565:                  (m_branchInst.function == SPECIAL_JR || m_branchInst.function == SPECIAL_JALR))
  566:         {
  567:             emitRegisterJump(m_branchInst.function == SPECIAL_JALR ? RegisterBranchKind::Call : RegisterBranchKind::Jump);
  568:         }
  569:         else if (m_branchInst.isBranch)
  570:         {
  571:             emitConditionalBranch();
  572:         }
  573:         else
  574:         {
  575:             emitFallbackInstruction();
  576:         }
  577: 
  578:         emitFallthroughLabelIfNeeded();
  579:         emitFinalFallthrough();
  580:         return m_ss.str();
  581:     }
  582: }

   53:     static std::string sanitizeIdentifierBody(const std::string &name)
   54:     {
   55:         std::string sanitized;
   56:         sanitized.reserve(name.size() + 1);
   57: 
   58:         for (char c : name)
   59:         {
   60:             const unsigned char uc = static_cast<unsigned char>(c);
   61:             if (std::isalnum(uc) || c == '_')
   62:             {
   63:                 sanitized.push_back(c);
   64:             }
   65:             else
   66:             {
   67:                 sanitized.push_back('_');
   68:             }
   69:         }
   70: 
   71:         if (sanitized.empty())
   72:         {
   73:             return sanitized;
   74:         }
   75: 
   76:         const unsigned char first = static_cast<unsigned char>(sanitized.front());
   77:         if (!(std::isalpha(first) || sanitized.front() == '_'))
   78:         {
   79:             sanitized.insert(sanitized.begin(), '_');
   80:         }
   81: 
   82:         return sanitized;
   83:     }
   84: 
   85:     static bool isReservedCxxIdentifier(const std::string &name)
   86:     {
   87:         if (name.size() >= 2 && name[0] == '_' && name[1] == '_')
   88:             return true;
   89:         if (name.size() >= 2 && name[0] == '_' && std::isupper(static_cast<unsigned char>(name[1])))
   90:             return true;
   91:         return false;
   92:     }
   93: 
   94:     static bool isReservedCxxKeyword(const std::string &name)
   95:     {
   96:         return kKeywords.contains(name);
   97:     }
   98:

  104:             m_symbols.emplace(symbol.address, symbol);
  105:         }
  106:     }

  123:     void CodeGenerator::setConfiguredJumpTables(const std::vector<JumpTable> &jumpTables)
  124:     {
  125:         m_configJumpTableTargetsByAddress.clear();
  126:         for (const auto &table : jumpTables)
  127:         {
  128:             auto &targets = m_configJumpTableTargetsByAddress[table.address];
  129:             for (const auto &entry : table.entries)
  130:             {
  131:                 targets.push_back(entry.target);
  132:             }
  133:         }
  134: 
  135:         for (auto &[address, targets] : m_configJumpTableTargetsByAddress)
  136:         {
  137:             (void)address;
  138:             std::sort(targets.begin(), targets.end());
  139:             targets.erase(std::unique(targets.begin(), targets.end()), targets.end());
  140:         }
  141:     }

  147:         {
  148:             (void)owner;
  149:             std::sort(targets.begin(), targets.end());
  150:             targets.erase(std::unique(targets.begin(), targets.end()), targets.end());
  151:         }

  164:     std::string CodeGenerator::getFunctionName(uint32_t address) const
  165:     {
  166:         auto it = m_renamedFunctions.find(address);
  167:         if (it != m_renamedFunctions.end())
  168:         {
  169:             return it->second;
  170:         }
  171: 
  172:         const Symbol *sym = findSymbolByAddress(address);
  173:         if (sym && sym->isFunction)
  174:         {
  175:             return CodeGenerator::sanitizeFunctionName(sym->name);
  176:         }
  177: 
  178:         return "";
  179:     }
  180: 
  181:     std::string CodeGenerator::sanitizeFunctionName(const std::string &name) const
  182:     {
  183:         std::string sanitized = sanitizeIdentifierBody(name);
  184:         if (sanitized.empty())
  185:             return sanitized;
  186: 
  187:         // ugly but will do for now
  188:         if (sanitized == "main")
  189:             return "ps2_main";
  190: 
  191:         if (isReservedCxxKeyword(sanitized))
  192:             return "ps2_" + sanitized;
  193: 
  194:         if (sanitized[0] == '_')
  195:             return "ps2" + sanitized;
  196: 
  197:         if (!isReservedCxxIdentifier(sanitized))
  198:             return sanitized;
  199: 
  200:         return "ps2_" + sanitized;
  201:     }

SOURCE FILE: ps2xTest\src\r5900_decoder_tests.cpp
LINES: 220
BYTES: 10728
    1: #include "MiniTest.h"
    2: #include "ps2recomp/r5900_decoder.h"
    3: 
    4: using namespace ps2recomp;
    5: 
    6: void register_r5900_decoder_tests()
    7: {
    8:     MiniTest::Case("R5900Decoder", [](TestCase &tc)
    9:                    {
   10:     tc.Run("decodes JAL with jump target and call flag", [](TestCase &t) {
   11:         // jal 0x00400000 at address 0x1000 => opcode 0x0C100000 (target = 0x00400000 >> 2)
   12:         uint32_t address = 0x1000;
   13:         uint32_t target = 0x00400000;
   14:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_JAL << 26) | ((target >> 2) & 0x03FFFFFF);
   15: 
   16:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   17:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
   18: 
   19:         t.IsTrue(inst.isJump, "jal should be marked as jump");
   20:         t.IsTrue(inst.isCall, "jal should be marked as call");
   21:         t.IsTrue(inst.hasDelaySlot, "jal has a delay slot");
   22:         t.Equals(decoder.getJumpTarget(inst), target, "jal jump target should match encoded target");
   23:     });
   24: 
   25:     tc.Run("J computes target with upper PC bits", [](TestCase &t) {
   26:         // Place J at address 0x8FFF_FFFC targeting 0x8123_4560 (upper bits from PC+4)
   27:         uint32_t address = 0x8FFFFFFC;
   28:         uint32_t encodedTarget = 0x0123456; // 0x048D1598 >> 2, but we want lower bits of 0x1234560
   29:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_J << 26) | (encodedTarget & 0x03FFFFFF);
   30: 
   31:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   32:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
   33: 
   34:         uint32_t expectedPcUpper = (address + 4) & 0xF0000000;
   35:         uint32_t expected = expectedPcUpper | (encodedTarget << 2);
   36:         t.Equals(decoder.getJumpTarget(inst), expected, "J target should combine PC upper bits with encoded target");
   37:     });
   38: 
   39:     tc.Run("JR/JALR jump target is zero (dynamic)", [](TestCase &t) {
   40:         uint32_t address = 0x1200;
   41:         uint32_t jrRaw = (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (2 << 21) | SPECIAL_JR;
   42:         uint32_t jalrRaw = (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (3 << 21) | (31 << 11) | SPECIAL_JALR;
   43: 
   44:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   45:         Instruction jr = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, jrRaw);
   46:         Instruction jalr = decoder.decodeInstruction(address + 4, jalrRaw);
   47: 
   48:         t.Equals(decoder.getJumpTarget(jr), 0u, "JR jump target should be unknown (0)");
   49:         t.Equals(decoder.getJumpTarget(jalr), 0u, "JALR jump target should be unknown (0)");
   50:     });
   51: 
   52:     tc.Run("decodes BEQ sets branch flags and target", [](TestCase &t) {
   53:         // beq r1, r2, offset 0x4 (word offset) at address 0x2000
   54:         uint32_t address = 0x2000;
   55:         uint16_t offset = 0x0004;
   56:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_BEQ << 26) | (1 << 21) | (2 << 16) | offset;
   57: 
   58:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   59:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
   60: 
   61:         t.IsTrue(inst.isBranch, "beq should be marked as branch");
   62:         t.IsTrue(inst.hasDelaySlot, "beq has a delay slot");
   63:         uint32_t expectedTarget = address + 4 + (static_cast<int16_t>(offset) << 2);
   64:         t.Equals(decoder.getBranchTarget(inst), expectedTarget, "beq target should be computed from simmediate");
   65:     });
   66: 
   67:     tc.Run("branch target sign-extends negative offset", [](TestCase &t) {
   68:         uint32_t address = 0x2100;
   69:         int16_t negOffset = -4; // jump back 16 bytes
   70:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_BNE << 26) | (1 << 21) | (2 << 16) | (negOffset & 0xFFFF);
   71: 
   72:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   73:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
   74: 
   75:         uint32_t expectedTarget = address + 4 + (static_cast<int16_t>(negOffset) << 2);
   76:         t.Equals(decoder.getBranchTarget(inst), expectedTarget, "negative branch offsets should sign-extend");
   77:     });
   78: 
   79:     tc.Run("decodes load/store flags", [](TestCase &t) {
   80:         uint32_t address = 0x3000;
   81:         uint32_t lwRaw = (OPCODE_LW << 26) | (1 << 21) | (2 << 16) | 0x10;
   82:         uint32_t swRaw = (OPCODE_SW << 26) | (3 << 21) | (4 << 16) | 0x20;
   83: 
   84:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   85:         Instruction lw = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, lwRaw);
   86:         Instruction sw = decoder.decodeInstruction(address + 4, swRaw);
   87: 
   88:         t.IsTrue(lw.isLoad, "lw should be marked as load");
   89:         t.IsFalse(lw.isStore, "lw should not be marked as store");
   90:         t.IsTrue(sw.isStore, "sw should be marked as store");
   91:         t.IsFalse(sw.isLoad, "sw should not be marked as load");
   92:     });
   93: 
   94:     tc.Run("JR is marked as return when rs is $ra", [](TestCase &t) {
   95:         uint32_t address = 0x4000;
   96:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (31 << 21) | SPECIAL_JR; // jr $ra
   97: 
   98:         R5900Decoder decoder;
   99:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  100: 
  101:         t.IsTrue(inst.isJump, "jr should be jump");
  102:         t.IsTrue(inst.isReturn, "jr $ra should be marked as return");
  103:         t.IsTrue(inst.hasDelaySlot, "jr has delay slot");
  104:     });
  105: 
  106:     tc.Run("JALR marks call and writes rd when non-zero", [](TestCase &t) {
  107:         uint32_t address = 0x5000;
  108:         uint32_t rd = 5;
  109:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (2 << 21) | (rd << 11) | SPECIAL_JALR; // jalr $v0, $a0
  110: 
  111:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  112:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  113: 
  114:         t.IsTrue(inst.isJump, "jalr should be jump");
  115:         t.IsTrue(inst.isCall, "jalr should be call");
  116:         t.IsTrue(inst.hasDelaySlot, "jalr has delay slot");
  117:         t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR, "jalr with rd!=0 should mark GPR modification");
  118:     });
  119: 
  120:     tc.Run("R5900 MULT marks rd modification when rd is non-zero", [](TestCase &t) {
  121:         uint32_t address = 0x5800;
  122:         uint32_t rawWithRd = (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (4 << 21) | (5 << 16) | (3 << 11) | SPECIAL_MULT;
  123:         uint32_t rawRdZero = (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (4 << 21) | (5 << 16) | (0 << 11) | SPECIAL_MULT;
  124: 
  125:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  126:         Instruction withRd = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, rawWithRd);
  127:         Instruction rdZero = decoder.decodeInstruction(address + 4, rawRdZero);
  128: 
  129:         t.IsTrue(withRd.modificationInfo.modifiesControl, "MULT should modify HI/LO");
  130:         t.IsTrue(withRd.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR, "MULT should mark rd modification when rd!=0");
  131:         t.IsFalse(rdZero.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR, "MULT should not mark rd modification when rd==0");
  132:     });
  133: 
  134:     tc.Run("R5900 MMI MULT1 marks rd modification when rd is non-zero", [](TestCase &t) {
  135:         uint32_t address = 0x5900;
  136:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_MMI << 26) | (6 << 21) | (7 << 16) | (8 << 11) | MMI_MULT1;
  137: 
  138:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  139:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  140: 
  141:         t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl, "MULT1 should modify HI1/LO1");
  142:         t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR, "MULT1 should mark rd modification when rd!=0");
  143:     });
  144: 
  145:     tc.Run("MMI instruction sets MMI flags", [](TestCase &t) {
  146:         uint32_t address = 0x6000;
  147:         // Use opcode 0x1C (MMI), rs=1, rt=2, rd=3, sa=MMI0_PADDW (0)
  148:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_MMI << 26) | (1 << 21) | (2 << 16) | (3 << 11) | MMI0_PADDW;
  149: 
  150:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  151:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  152: 
  153:         t.IsTrue(inst.isMMI, "MMI opcode should set isMMI");
  154:         t.IsTrue(inst.isMultimedia, "MMI opcode should set multimedia flag");
  155:         t.Equals(inst.mmiType, static_cast<uint8_t>(0), "MMI0 should set mmiType to 0");
  156:         t.Equals(inst.mmiFunction, static_cast<uint8_t>(MMI0_PADDW), "MMI function should match sa field");
  157:     });
  158: 
  159:     tc.Run("COP2 VU macro op marks VU flags", [](TestCase &t) {
  160:         uint32_t address = 0x7000;
  161:         uint8_t s2op = VU0_S2_VDIV;          // 0x38
  162:         uint8_t fhi  = s2op >> 2;             // 0x0E -> bits[10:6]
  163:         uint8_t flo  = s2op & 0x3;            // 0x00 -> bits[1:0]
  164:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_COP2 << 26) | (COP2_CO << 21) | (fhi << 6) | (0x3C | flo);
  165: 
  166:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  167:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  168: 
  169:         t.IsTrue(inst.isVU, "VU macro should set isVU");
  170:         t.IsTrue(inst.isMultimedia, "VU macro should set multimedia");
  171:         t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl, "VDIV should mark control modification");
  172:         t.IsTrue(inst.vectorInfo.usesQReg, "VDIV should use Q register");
  173:         uint8_t expectedVecField = static_cast<uint8_t>((raw >> 21) & 0xF);
  174:         t.Equals(inst.vectorInfo.vectorField, expectedVecField, "vector field should reflect encoding");
  175:     });
  176: 
  177:     tc.Run("REGIMM branch and link marks call and GPR modification", [](TestCase &t) {
  178:         uint32_t address = 0x8000;
  179:         uint16_t offset = 0x2;
  180:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_REGIMM << 26) | (1 << 21) | (REGIMM_BGEZAL << 16) | offset;
  181: 
  182:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  183:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  184: 
  185:         t.IsTrue(inst.isBranch, "bgezal should be branch");
  186:         t.IsTrue(inst.isCall, "bgezal should be call (link)");
  187:         t.IsTrue(inst.hasDelaySlot, "bgezal has delay slot");
  188:         t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR, "bgezal should mark GPR modification for $ra");
  189:         uint32_t expectedTarget = address + 4 + (static_cast<int16_t>(offset) << 2);
  190:         t.Equals(decoder.getBranchTarget(inst), expectedTarget, "bgezal target should be computed");
  191:     });
  192: 
  193:     tc.Run("LL/SC modify control and set load/store flags", [](TestCase &t) {
  194:         uint32_t address = 0x9000;
  195:         uint32_t llRaw = (OPCODE_LL << 26) | (2 << 21) | (3 << 16) | 0x10;
  196:         uint32_t scRaw = (OPCODE_SC << 26) | (4 << 21) | (5 << 16) | 0x20;
  197: 
  198:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  199:         Instruction ll = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, llRaw);
  200:         Instruction sc = decoder.decodeInstruction(address + 4, scRaw);
  201: 
  202:         t.IsTrue(ll.isLoad, "ll should be load");
  203:         t.IsTrue(ll.modificationInfo.modifiesControl, "ll should modify control (LL bit)");
  204:         t.IsTrue(sc.isStore, "sc should be store");
  205:         t.IsTrue(sc.modificationInfo.modifiesControl, "sc should modify control (LL bit)");
  206:         t.IsTrue(sc.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR, "sc writes success flag to rt");
  207:     });
  208: 
  209:     tc.Run("COP0 ERET is marked as return without delay slot", [](TestCase &t) {
  210:         uint32_t address = 0xA000;
  211:         uint32_t raw = (OPCODE_COP0 << 26) | (COP0_CO << 21) | COP0_CO_ERET;
  212: 
  213:         R5900Decoder decoder;
  214:         Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(address, raw);
  215: 
  216:         t.IsTrue(inst.isReturn, "eret should be marked as return");
  217:         t.IsFalse(inst.hasDelaySlot, "eret should not have a delay slot");
  218:         t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl, "eret changes control state");
  219:     }); });
  220: }

  303:         gMpegStreamCallbackType.store(type, std::memory_order_release);
  304:         gMpegStreamCallbackDataAddr.store(dataAddr, std::memory_order_release);
  305:         gMpegStreamCallbackLen.store(len, std::memory_order_release);
  306:         gMpegStreamCallbackUserData.store(::getRegU32(ctx, 6), std::memory_order_release);
  307:         gMpegStreamCallbackCount.fetch_add(1u, std::memory_order_acq_rel);

  313: 
  314: void register_ps2_runtime_expansion_tests()
  315: {
  316:     MiniTest::Case("PS2RuntimeExpansion", [](TestCase &tc)
  317:     {
  318:         tc.Run("differential decoder/codegen gpr-write contract for MULT and DIV families", [](TestCase &t)
  319:         {
  320:             R5900Decoder decoder;
  321:             CodeGenerator generator({}, {});
  322: 
  323:             const struct
  324:             {
  325:                 const char *name;
  326:                 uint32_t raw;
  327:             } cases[] = {
  328:                 {"MULT rd!=0", (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (4u << 21) | (5u << 16) | (3u << 11) | SPECIAL_MULT},
  329:                 {"MULT rd==0", (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (4u << 21) | (5u << 16) | (0u << 11) | SPECIAL_MULT},
  330:                 {"DIV rd!=0", (OPCODE_SPECIAL << 26) | (6u << 21) | (7u << 16) | (9u << 11) | SPECIAL_DIV},
  331:                 {"MMI MULT1 rd!=0", (OPCODE_MMI << 26) | (8u << 21) | (9u << 16) | (10u << 11) | MMI_MULT1},
  332:                 {"MMI DIV1 rd!=0", (OPCODE_MMI << 26) | (8u << 21) | (9u << 16) | (10u << 11) | MMI_DIV1},
  333:             };
  334: 
  335:             for (size_t i = 0; i < std::size(cases); ++i)
  336:             {
  337:                 const Instruction inst = decoder.decodeInstruction(0x1000u + static_cast<uint32_t>(i * 4u), cases[i].raw);
  338:                 const std::string generated = generator.translateInstruction(inst);
  339:                 const bool emittedRdWrite = hasSignedRdWrite(generated, inst.rd);
  340: 
  341:                 t.Equals(emittedRdWrite, inst.modificationInfo.modifiesGPR,
  342:                          std::string("decoder/codegen mismatch for ") + cases[i].name);
  343:                 t.IsTrue(inst.modificationInfo.modifiesControl,
  344:                          std::string("HI/LO control side-effect missing for ") + cases[i].name);
  345:             }
  346:         });
  347: 
  348:         tc.Run("lookupFunction rejects internal resume PCs without exact registration", [](TestCase &t)
  349:         {
  350:             PS2Runtime runtime;
  351:             runtime.setMissingFunctionPolicy(PS2Runtime::MissingFunctionPolicy::Stop);
  352:             runtime.registerFunction(0x1000u, &testResumeOwnerFallbackHandler);
  353:             runtime.registerFunction(0x1100u, &testResumeNextFunctionHandler);
  354: 
  355:             R5900Context ctx{};
  356:             ctx.pc = 0x1010u;
  357:             auto fn = runtime.lookupFunction(ctx.pc);
  358:             fn(nullptr, &ctx, &runtime);
  359: 
  360:             t.Equals(::getRegU32(&ctx, 2), 0u,
  361:                      "unregistered resume PC should not alias to the nearest owner");
  362:             t.IsTrue(runtime.isStopRequested(),
  363:                      "missing exact dispatch target should request runtime stop");
  364:         });
  365: 
  366:         tc.Run("lookupFunction rejects final-function PCs inside code regions without exact registration", [](TestCase &t)

  551:         tc.Run("sceMpegDemuxPssRing dispatches registered video and audio stream callbacks", [](TestCase &t)
  552:         {
  553:             PS2Runtime runtime;
  554:             std::vector<uint8_t> rdram(PS2_RAM_SIZE, 0u);
  555:             ps2_stubs::resetMpegStubState();
  556: 
  557:             constexpr uint32_t kMpegAddr = 0x00123000u;
  558:             constexpr uint32_t kCallbackEntry = 0x00124000u;
  559:             constexpr uint32_t kVideoUserData = 0x11223344u;
  560:             constexpr uint32_t kAudioUserData = 0x55667788u;
  561:             constexpr uint32_t kVideoPacketAddr = 0x00128000u;
  562:             constexpr uint32_t kAudioPacketAddr = 0x00129000u;
  563: 
  564:             runtime.registerFunction(kCallbackEntry, &testRecordMpegStreamCallback);
  565:             runtime.registerFunction(kMpegCallbackStopPc, &testStopAfterMpegCallback);
  566:             auto prepareCallbackDispatch = [&]()
  567:             {
  568:                 R5900Context idleContext{};
  569:                 idleContext.pc = kMpegCallbackStopPc;
  570:                 runtime.eeScheduler().reset(rdram.data(), idleContext);
  571:             };
  572: 
  573:             auto registerGenericCallback = [&](uint32_t callbackType, uint32_t userData)
  574:             {
  575:                 R5900Context addCtx{};
  576:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  577:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 5, callbackType);
  578:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 6, kCallbackEntry);
  579:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 7, userData);
  580:                 ps2_stubs::sceMpegAddCallback(rdram.data(), &addCtx, &runtime);
  581:             };
  582: 
  583:             auto registerStreamCallback = [&](uint32_t streamType, uint32_t userData)
  584:             {
  585:                 R5900Context addCtx{};
  586:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  587:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 5, streamType);
  588:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 6, 0u);
  589:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 7, kCallbackEntry);
  590:                 setRegU32(addCtx, 8, userData);
  591:                 ps2_stubs::sceMpegAddStrCallback(rdram.data(), &addCtx, &runtime);
  592:             };
  593: 
  594:             auto writePesPacket = [&](uint32_t addr, uint8_t streamId, const std::vector<uint8_t> &payload)
  595:             {
  596:                 const uint16_t packetLen = static_cast<uint16_t>(payload.size() + 3u);
  597:                 std::vector<uint8_t> packet = {
  598:                     0x00u, 0x00u, 0x01u, streamId,
  599:                     static_cast<uint8_t>(packetLen >> 8u),
  600:                     static_cast<uint8_t>(packetLen & 0xFFu),
  601:                     0x80u, 0x00u, 0x00u};
  602:                 packet.insert(packet.end(), payload.begin(), payload.end());
  603:                 std::memcpy(rdram.data() + addr, packet.data(), packet.size());
  604:                 return static_cast<uint32_t>(packet.size());
  605:             };
  606: 
  607:             registerGenericCallback(0u, 0xDEAD0000u);
  608:             registerGenericCallback(2u, 0xDEAD0002u);
  609:             registerStreamCallback(0u, kVideoUserData);
  610:             registerStreamCallback(2u, kAudioUserData);
  611: 
  612:             const std::vector<uint8_t> videoPayload = {
  613:                 0x00u, 0x00u, 0x01u, 0xB3u, 0x14u, 0x00u, 0xF0u, 0x13u};
  614:             const uint32_t videoPacketSize = writePesPacket(kVideoPacketAddr, 0xE0u, videoPayload);
  615: 
  616:             gMpegStreamCallbackCount.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  617:             prepareCallbackDispatch();
  618:             R5900Context videoDemuxCtx{};
  619:             setRegU32(videoDemuxCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  620:             setRegU32(videoDemuxCtx, 5, kVideoPacketAddr);
  621:             setRegU32(videoDemuxCtx, 6, videoPacketSize);
  622:             setRegU32(videoDemuxCtx, 7, kVideoPacketAddr);
  623:             setRegU32(videoDemuxCtx, 8, videoPacketSize);
  624:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegDemuxPssRing(rdram.data(), &videoDemuxCtx, &runtime);
  625:             runtime.eeScheduler().run();
  626: 
  627:             t.Equals(getRegS32(videoDemuxCtx, 2), static_cast<int32_t>(videoPacketSize),
  628:                      "sceMpegDemuxPssRing should consume the video PES packet");
  629:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackCount.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 1u,
  630:                      "registered video stream callback should be invoked");
  631:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackMpeg.load(std::memory_order_acquire), kMpegAddr,
  632:                      "video callback should receive the MPEG handle");
  633:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackType.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 0u,
  634:                      "video callback data should report M2V stream type");
  635:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackDataAddr.load(std::memory_order_acquire), kVideoPacketAddr + 9u,
  636:                      "video callback data should point at PES payload");
  637:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackLen.load(std::memory_order_acquire),
  638:                      static_cast<uint32_t>(videoPayload.size()),
  639:                      "video callback data should report PES payload length");
  640:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackUserData.load(std::memory_order_acquire), kVideoUserData,
  641:                      "video callback should receive registered user data");
  642: 
  643:             const std::vector<uint8_t> audioPayload = {0x80u, 0x01u, 0x02u, 0x03u, 0x04u, 0x05u};
  644:             const uint32_t audioPacketSize = writePesPacket(kAudioPacketAddr, 0xBDu, audioPayload);
  645: 
  646:             gMpegStreamCallbackCount.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  647:             prepareCallbackDispatch();
  648:             R5900Context audioDemuxCtx{};
  649:             setRegU32(audioDemuxCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  650:             setRegU32(audioDemuxCtx, 5, kAudioPacketAddr);
  651:             setRegU32(audioDemuxCtx, 6, audioPacketSize);
  652:             setRegU32(audioDemuxCtx, 7, kAudioPacketAddr);
  653:             setRegU32(audioDemuxCtx, 8, audioPacketSize);
  654:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegDemuxPssRing(rdram.data(), &audioDemuxCtx, &runtime);
  655:             runtime.eeScheduler().run();
  656: 
  657:             t.Equals(getRegS32(audioDemuxCtx, 2), static_cast<int32_t>(audioPacketSize),
  658:                      "sceMpegDemuxPssRing should consume the audio PES packet");
  659:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackCount.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 1u,
  660:                      "registered audio stream callback should be invoked");
  661:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackType.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 2u,
  662:                      "audio callback data should report PCM stream type");
  663:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackDataAddr.load(std::memory_order_acquire), kAudioPacketAddr + 9u,
  664:                      "audio callback data should point at PES payload");
  665:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackLen.load(std::memory_order_acquire),
  666:                      static_cast<uint32_t>(audioPayload.size()),
  667:                      "audio callback data should report PES payload length");
  668:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackUserData.load(std::memory_order_acquire), kAudioUserData,
  669:                      "audio callback should receive registered user data");
  670: 
  671:             ps2_stubs::notifyMpegCdStreamEof();
  672: 
  673:             gMpegStreamCallbackCount.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  674:             R5900Context afterEofDemuxCtx{};
  675:             setRegU32(afterEofDemuxCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  676:             setRegU32(afterEofDemuxCtx, 5, kVideoPacketAddr);
  677:             setRegU32(afterEofDemuxCtx, 6, videoPacketSize);
  678:             setRegU32(afterEofDemuxCtx, 7, kVideoPacketAddr);
  679:             setRegU32(afterEofDemuxCtx, 8, videoPacketSize);
  680:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegDemuxPssRing(rdram.data(), &afterEofDemuxCtx, &runtime);
  681: 
  682:             t.Equals(getRegS32(afterEofDemuxCtx, 2), static_cast<int32_t>(videoPacketSize),
  683:                      "post-EOF demux should continue consuming caller data");
  684:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackCount.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 0u,
  685:                      "post-EOF demux should not feed callbacks again");
  686: 
  687:             R5900Context resetCtx{};
  688:             setRegU32(resetCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  689:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegReset(rdram.data(), &resetCtx, &runtime);
  690: 
  691:             gMpegStreamCallbackCount.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  692:             R5900Context afterResetDemuxCtx{};
  693:             setRegU32(afterResetDemuxCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  694:             setRegU32(afterResetDemuxCtx, 5, kVideoPacketAddr);
  695:             setRegU32(afterResetDemuxCtx, 6, videoPacketSize);
  696:             setRegU32(afterResetDemuxCtx, 7, kVideoPacketAddr);
  697:             setRegU32(afterResetDemuxCtx, 8, videoPacketSize);
  698:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegDemuxPssRing(rdram.data(), &afterResetDemuxCtx, &runtime);
  699: 
  700:             t.Equals(getRegS32(afterResetDemuxCtx, 2), static_cast<int32_t>(videoPacketSize),
  701:                      "post-EOF reset demux should still drain caller data");
  702:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackCount.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 0u,
  703:                      "post-EOF reset demux should not restart callbacks on stale data");
  704: 
  705:             ps2_stubs::notifyMpegCdStreamStart();
  706: 
  707:             gMpegStreamCallbackCount.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  708:             prepareCallbackDispatch();
  709:             R5900Context afterNewStreamDemuxCtx{};
  710:             setRegU32(afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  711:             setRegU32(afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, 5, kVideoPacketAddr);
  712:             setRegU32(afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, 6, videoPacketSize);
  713:             setRegU32(afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, 7, kVideoPacketAddr);
  714:             setRegU32(afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, 8, videoPacketSize);
  715:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegDemuxPssRing(rdram.data(), &afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, &runtime);
  716:             runtime.eeScheduler().run();
  717: 
  718:             t.Equals(getRegS32(afterNewStreamDemuxCtx, 2), static_cast<int32_t>(videoPacketSize),
  719:                      "new CD stream demux should reopen an ended MPEG handle");
  720:             t.Equals(gMpegStreamCallbackCount.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 1u,
  721:                      "new CD stream demux should allow callbacks on a reused MPEG handle");
  722: 
  723:             constexpr uint32_t kMpegWorkAddr = 0x00130000u;
  724:             R5900Context createCtx{};
  725:             setRegU32(createCtx, 4, kMpegAddr);
  726:             setRegU32(createCtx, 5, kMpegWorkAddr);
  727:             setRegU32(createCtx, 6, 0x2000u);
  728:             ps2_stubs::sceMpegCreate(rdram.data(), &createCtx, &runtime);
  729:             t.IsTrue(::getRegU32(&createCtx, 2) != 0u,

  749:         tc.Run("sceMpegGetPicture blocks as a typed scheduler wait and resumes on EOF", [](TestCase &t)

  757:             runtime.registerFunction(kMpegWaitProducerPc, testMpegWaitProducer);
  758:             gMpegWaitResult.store(-999, std::memory_order_release);

  769:                      "MPEG producer guest thread should become ready");

  773:                      "EOF completion should resume GetPicture with success");
  774:             t.Equals(gMpegWaitStage.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 3u,
  775:                      "MPEG waiter should reach its continuation after the producer posts EOF");
  776:             t.Equals(Ps2FastRead32(rdram.data(), kMpegWaitHandle + 0x00u), 320u,
  777:                      "resumed GetPicture should publish the configured width");

  779:                      "resumed GetPicture should publish the configured height");

  788:             ps2_stubs::enqueueMpegDecodedFrameForTesting(kMpegNoDuplicateHandle);
  789:             runtime.registerFunction(kMpegNoDuplicateMainPc, testMpegNoDuplicateMain);
  790:             runtime.registerFunction(kMpegNoDuplicateResumePc, testMpegNoDuplicateResume);
  791:             runtime.registerFunction(kMpegNoDuplicateProducerPc, testMpegNoDuplicateProducer);
  792:             gMpegNoDuplicateStage.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  793:             gMpegNoDuplicateProducerStage.store(0u, std::memory_order_release);
  794: 
  795:             R5900Context mainContext{};
  796:             mainContext.pc = kMpegNoDuplicateMainPc;
  797:             EeScheduler &ee = runtime.eeScheduler();
  798:             ee.reset(rdram.data(), mainContext);
  799:             const int producerId = ee.createThread(EeThreadCreateParams{
  800:                 0u, kMpegNoDuplicateProducerPc, 0u, 0u, 0u, 10, 0u});
  801:             t.IsTrue(producerId > 1, "MPEG producer guest thread should be created");

  803:                      "MPEG producer guest thread should become ready");
