  805: 
  806:             t.Equals(gMpegNoDuplicateProducerStage.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 1u,
  807:                      "the producer should run while the second GetPicture is waiting");
  808:             t.Equals(gMpegNoDuplicateStage.load(std::memory_order_acquire), 3u,
  809:                      "EOF should resume the blocked GetPicture continuation");
  810:             t.Equals(Ps2FastRead32(rdram.data(), kMpegNoDuplicateHandle + 0x08u), 1u,
  811:                      "only the injected decoder frame should be counted as served");
  812:         });
  813: 
  814:         tc.Run("sceSdRemote isolates voice transfers from block streaming state", [](TestCase &t)
  815:         {
  816:             std::vector<uint8_t> rdram(PS2_RAM_SIZE, 0u);
  817:             constexpr uint32_t kStackAddr = 0x00100000u;
  818:             constexpr uint32_t kBlockBase = 0x00012340u;
  819:             constexpr uint32_t kBlockSize = 0x00003000u;
  820:             constexpr uint32_t kBlockPause = 0x00012740u;
  821: 
  822:             R5900Context initCtx{};
  823:             ps2_stubs::sceSdRemoteInit(rdram.data(), &initCtx, nullptr);
  824: 
  825:             R5900Context blockCtx{};
  826:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 29, kStackAddr);
  827:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 4, 1u);
  828:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 5, 0x80E0u);
  829:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 6, 1u);
  830:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 7, 0x13u);
  831:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 8, kBlockBase);
  832:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 9, kBlockSize);
  833:             setRegU32(blockCtx, 10, kBlockPause);
  834:             ps2_stubs::sceSdRemote(rdram.data(), &blockCtx, nullptr);
  835: 
  836:             R5900Context blockStatusCtx{};
  837:             setRegU32(blockStatusCtx, 29, kStackAddr);
  838:             setRegU32(blockStatusCtx, 4, 1u);
  839:             setRegU32(blockStatusCtx, 5, 0x8100u);
  840:             setRegU32(blockStatusCtx, 6, 1u);
  841:             setRegU32(blockStatusCtx, 7, 0u);
  842:             ps2_stubs::sceSdRemote(rdram.data(), &blockStatusCtx, nullptr);
  843:             t.Equals(getRegU32(&blockStatusCtx, 2), 0x00012B40u,
  844:                      "initial block-status poll should advance the streaming ring");
  845: 
  846:             R5900Context voiceCtx{};
  847:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 29, kStackAddr);
  848:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 4, 1u);
  849:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 5, 0x80D0u);
  850:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 6, 0u);
  851:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 7, 0u);
  852:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 8, 0x00022000u);
  853:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 9, 0x00004000u);
  854:             setRegU32(voiceCtx, 10, 0x00000800u);
  855:             ps2_stubs::sceSdRemote(rdram.data(), &voiceCtx, nullptr);
  856:             t.Equals(getRegU32(&voiceCtx, 2), 0x00000800u,
  857:                      "DMA voice transfer should report its transferred byte count");
  858: 
  859:             R5900Context voiceStatusCtx{};
  860:             setRegU32(voiceStatusCtx, 29, kStackAddr);
  861:             setRegU32(voiceStatusCtx, 4, 1u);
  862:             setRegU32(voiceStatusCtx, 5, 0x80F0u);
  863:             setRegU32(voiceStatusCtx, 6, 0u);
  864:             setRegU32(voiceStatusCtx, 7, 1u);
  865:             ps2_stubs::sceSdRemote(rdram.data(), &voiceStatusCtx, nullptr);
  866:             t.Equals(getRegU32(&voiceStatusCtx, 2), 1u,
  867:                      "voice-transfer status should complete independently from block position");
  868: 
  869:             ps2_stubs::sceSdRemote(rdram.data(), &blockStatusCtx, nullptr);
  870:             t.Equals(getRegU32(&blockStatusCtx, 2), 0x00012F40u,
  871:                      "voice transfer should not replace or advance the block-streaming ring");
  872:         });

  875:         {
  876:             std::vector<uint8_t> rdram(PS2_RAM_SIZE, 0u);
  877:             constexpr uint32_t kStackAddr = 0x00100000u;
  878:

  883:                               uint32_t core,

  886:                               uint32_t arg5 = 0u,
  887:                               uint32_t arg6 = 0u)

  897:                 setRegU32(ctx, 10, arg6);
